# Nhóm B (AI quality) — Implementation Plan

> **Status 30/10/2026:** written after the owner said "viết plan" on the revised design, and reshaped to **one plan executed sequentially** ("gộp lại thành một plan
> đi, mình sẽ cho làm tuần tự"; design §3, E12). Reviewed independently on 29/10/2026 ([`../reviews/2026-10-29-nhom-b-plan-review.md`](../reviews/2026-10-29-nhom-b-plan-review.md),
> P1–P21; its last section says how each finding was handled). The four questions added after the reviews were settled on 30/10/2026 (design §0, D37–D40) and are
> written in: D37 and D38 in M2 (M2-T2, T5, T7, T10, T13; M3-T12 keeps them), D40 in M2-T6, M2-T9, M3-T7 and M3-T15, D39 as **Milestone M6** (M6-T1…T9, not
> covered by the 29/10 review). The former M2-T14 (waiting for Q-R1) is gone: D37 lives where the gate is written. Every milestone and the closing tasks are written;
> no owner question is open (end of this header).

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:executing-plans (sequential, one milestone after another) or superpowers:subagent-driven-development to
> implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** changing an AI operation's prompt or model becomes draft → eval on a golden set with a hidden held-out part → rule, expected-answer and judge scores → compare
with the live config → publish (warnings) → rollback; the admin sees how good the real AI questions of the last 7 days are, and whether learners keep learning (D7/D30).

**Architecture:** module `ai` owns examples, frozen sets, runs, configs, judges, passes, scores and labels; Practice registers its rules into `ai` through a port at
`onModuleInit` and selects real questions for judging; Content gives catalog forms by lemma; Identity gives staff ids and registrations; Settings gives a setting's value at a
past instant. No module reads another module's tables (D3). The admin web gets the "Cấu hình", "Judge", "Gắn nhãn", "Câu AI thật" screens and new dashboard blocks.

**Tech Stack:** API NestJS `^12.0.1`, Prisma `7.10.0` (TypedSQL), zod `4.6.5`, Vitest `^4.1.2`, BullMQ (existing `ai-eval` queue). Admin React `19.3.0`, react-router
`8.4.0`, TanStack Query `5.104.2`, use-intl `4.14.9`, react-aria-components `1.22.1`, Vitest `4.1.11`, Playwright `1.63.0`. **No new dependency.**

**Spec:** [`../specs/2026-10-29-nhom-b-chat-luong-ai-design.md`](../specs/2026-10-29-nhom-b-chat-luong-ai-design.md) ("design §N", cases "B#", proposals "E#"). Decisions:
[`../decisions/2026-10-29-nhom-b-chat-luong-ai-decisions.md`](../decisions/2026-10-29-nhom-b-chat-luong-ai-decisions.md). Shared names:
[`2026-10-29-nhom-b-00-contract.md`](2026-10-29-nhom-b-00-contract.md) ("contract §N"). Code facts:
[`../../preparation/NHOM_B_CODE_FACTS_2026-10-29.md`](../../preparation/NHOM_B_CODE_FACTS_2026-10-29.md) (F1–F12). Design review:
[`../reviews/2026-10-29-nhom-b-design-review.md`](../reviews/2026-10-29-nhom-b-design-review.md) (R1–R22). Line numbers are `origin/dev` @ `9b5288f`; every milestone
rewrites code the next one reads, so **read the code again when a task starts**.

| M3 Judge | K2 / P2 | `feat/nhom-b-m3-judge` | M3-T1…T21 | M3-a (T1–T6), M3-b (T7–T13), M3-c (T14–T21) |

and a verification task; the round ends with the closing tasks (design §13 "Xong khi").

## Global Constraints

- **Module boundaries (D3):** `src/ai/**` never imports `src/content/**` or `src/practice/**`; Practice and Content import only `ai/index.ts`; only Identity touches
  Identity tables; Entitlements never reads `app_setting_changes` (Settings does). New user-id columns are mapped in `src/identity/deletion/account-data-map.ts`.
- **Names:** contract §2–§7 exactly; a milestone adds a name to the contract when a later milestone uses it.
- **Permissions (D19):** every new write and every new AI admin read needs `settings.manage`; dashboard reads keep `ai.view`.
- **`/v1` additive-only**, with the one named exception (design §6.6: the two `PUT`s return 404 after M2-c); extended responses add fields, never change existing ones.
- **Held-out never leaks** (design §5.1, R7) on any surface a milestone adds; **the judge is blind** (design §5.4): no model, provider, config number, run id or
  `operation_id` in what it receives; **private words never leave Practice** (design §7.1, R6).
- **Learners see no change at any deploy** until an admin publishes a new config (design §6.1 migration).
- **Tests:** default `test` commands are unit / component only (no Docker); SQL behaviour and races are proven by migrations on data and by the smoke (E19). Every test pins
  exact values. Admin tests are behavioural (role and visible text).
- **Copy:** new admin strings in `messages/vi.json` and `messages/en.json` with equal keys (`src/i18n/parity.test.ts`); design-fixed Vietnamese copy verbatim; other vi copy
  is chosen by this plan and quoted in the tasks.
- **New settings:** every task that adds a setting (M1-T2, M3-T2, M4-T5, M6-T2) also adds its default and bounds to the admin mock `apps/admin/e2e/support/settings.ts`, or the
  mocked e2e of the "Cài đặt hệ thống" page fails.
- **Paths:** inside a task a path without `apps/` is under `apps/api/` (API files) or `apps/admin/` (`src/features/**`, `src/app/**`, `e2e/**`, `e2e-real/**`, `messages/**`).
- **Before each push** (CI, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`), the steps of every app touched, each exiting 0. **api:** on a fresh DB `pnpm --filter api db:setup`;
  `pnpm --filter api exec prisma migrate diff --from-config-datasource --to-schema prisma --exit-code --script`; `pnpm --filter api lint`;
  `pnpm --filter api exec tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json`; `pnpm --filter api build`; `pnpm --filter api test`. **admin:** `pnpm --filter admin lint`;
  `pnpm --filter admin typecheck`; `pnpm --filter admin test`; `pnpm --filter admin build` with `VITE_API_BASE_URL=https://admin-api.wordmet.com` (CI's production host
  guard); `pnpm --filter admin build:e2e && pnpm --filter admin test:e2e`; `pnpm --filter admin test:e2e:real` against a real API and worker started as CI's
  `admin-e2e-real` job does (`AI_FAKE_ENABLED=true`, `MAINTENANCE_ENABLED=false`, fresh DB). Scan for secrets. A defect found on the way is 2 commits (red test, then fix).
- **The smoke run** (every verification task; as `docs/deliveries/buoc-7.md:98-127`): `docker compose up -d`; create database `english_learning_smoke_nb`; in `apps/api` with
  `DATABASE_URL` on it, `AI_FAKE_ENABLED=true`, no `OPENAI_API_KEY` / `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, `JOB_QUEUE_PREFIX=wordmet-nb-smoke`: `pnpm db:setup`, `pnpm build`, `pnpm run:dev`;
  then `pnpm smoke:nhom-b`. Twice, the database dropped and created again in between; it passes when both runs exit 0 with every step of every part `ok`. A step failing on
  a code defect is fixed the repo's way: a red unit test in the owning task's spec, then the fix, then the smoke again. Each verification task appends its run to
  `docs/deliveries/nhom-b-smoke-output.txt` under "M<n>"; C-T1 replaces the file with the final run (contract §7). **Old smokes** (design §13): `pnpm smoke:buoc-6` and
  `pnpm smoke:buoc-7`, each on a fresh database with its documented env; from M2 on also `pnpm learner:setup` as in M2-T10.
- **`ai.test` budget:** 20 hits per hour per user (`src/common/config/app-config.ts:142`), one per eval run and per prompt or draft test (`src/ai/eval/eval-runs.service.ts:89`);
  judge passes do not count. `smoke-nhom-b.mjs` runs `setup()`, `partM1()`, `partM2()` as its first admin and every later part as a new admin made with
  `POST /v1/admin/users` (`staff_role: 'admin'`, no registration spent); the script's header says so.

## Review Focus (plan-wide; each milestone adds its own)

1. A held-out example shown anywhere new (judge reasons, labels, compare, calls page, real-question page) → only counts, never text (M1, M3).
2. Anything the judge receives containing a model id, provider name or config number → never (M3-T4, M4-T4).
3. An admin eval / judge burst on the same day as learners practise → learners keep AI (B21; M3-T5).
4. A private word in any MCQ candidate or wrong meaning → that question is never judged nor copied into examples (B39; M4-T4).
5. Deploying M2-c on a DB with real prompts and models → identical prompt text, model, effort, tokens and `prompt_revision` numbers in new `ai_calls` (M2-T8).

## Merge notes

The milestones were planned separately from the contract; what merging them reconciled (formerly table X1–X12) is now written into the tasks:
- Status `today`: M3-T5 makes `calls` count learner calls and adds `admin_cost_usd`, `admin_usd_cap`; M5 only reads them, and M5-T9 owns the dashboard's admin USD meter.
- Dashboard calibration reads M3's `JudgeAgreement.forRevisions` (M5-T3); the dashboard type is `DashboardJudgeRevision` (M3 keeps `JudgeRevisionView`).
- The failing fake judge is `fake:bad-form` (M3-T4); there is no `fake:judge-fail`.
- A real-question pass carries its operation in `filters.operation` (M3-T8); M4 reuses M3's admin client, label control and copy (M4-T9, M4-T10).
- `ai_eval_runs.hidden_at` marks a "deleted" run that holds labels; the publish gate (M3-T11) and the dashboard (M5-T4, M5-T5) skip it. An `eval_item` subject id is
  `ai_eval_run_items.id`.
- Columns and problem types beyond design §4 and contract §6 are defined in the task that adds them (M1-T3, M2-T1, M3-T3…T5) and listed in each milestone's constraints.

## Open owner questions

None. Q-R1, Q-R1b, Q-R3 (design §0) and Q1 (plan review P12) were answered on 30/10/2026 as D37–D40 (decision record); the header says where each is planned.

`ai_models.hidden` (M2-T1, toggle on the Models page in M2-T6); pickers hide a model that is `hidden` **or** provider-`retired`, except the one currently selected

## Milestone M1 — Golden set (design K1)

**Goal:** each AI operation's example set gets an expected answer per example, a hidden dev / held-out split, frozen versions, Practice's rules inside eval,
snapshot results per example (E1), and a comparison of two runs on the same version. Everything stays in `src/ai/eval/**` except the rules port: `ai` declares
`EvalRules` and a registry, Practice registers `PracticeEvalRules` at `onModuleInit` (precedent `src/practice/entry-usage.probe.ts:16-18`) and reads catalog forms
through one new Content function. The admin keeps the `/ai/eval` page and its dialogs. Review: R7, R8, R14, R17, R21.

**Constraints (M1):**
- `ai/index.ts` exports `type EvalRules`, `type RuleResult`, `type ExpectedForms` for Practice; in `src/ai/**` only `ai.constants.spec.ts` imports Content or Practice
  (constants). Only `ai` reads `ai_eval_*`; only Content reads `entries` / `senses`.
- **Problem types:** contract §6 (`eval-set-incomplete` 422 with `missing_expected: string[]`, `no_held_out: boolean`; `eval-set-mismatch` 409; `example-not-held-out` 409)
  and M1-only `expected-invalid` 422 (`violations: string[]`, B2), `example-held-out` 409 (PATCH of a hidden example), `eval-set-conflict` 409 (B37). Existing ones
  unchanged: `validation-failed` 400, `not-found` 404.
- **Endpoints** on `AdminAiEvalController` (`settings.manage`, D19); writes carry `@RateLimit('user.write')`; `POST …/runs` keeps `@Idempotent()`.
- **Expected answer (design §5.1):** required for `generate_cloze`, `generate_meaning_question` (`{"forms": string[]}`, 1–50 non-empty strings), `lookup_word`
  (`{"lemma", "part_of_speech"}`, lemma 1–`LEMMA_MAX`, part of speech in `AI_VALID_POS`), `check_private_word` (`{"verdict"}` ∈ `PRIVATE_WORD_VERDICTS`); must be absent or
  `null` for `translate_sentence`, `generate_sentence_mcq`.
- **Split rule (design §5.1, E10, R21):** `nextSplit(heldOut, total, ratio)` = `held_out` when `total = 0`, else `held_out` iff `heldOut × 100 < round(ratio × 100) × total`;
  counted over the working set of the operation. Setting `ai.evalHeldOutRatio` default `0.3`, bounds `0.1`–`0.5`, at most two decimals. A `dev` example never becomes `held_out`.
- **Held-out stays hidden (design §5.1, R7):** input, output, `expected`, `note` of an unrevealed held-out example never leave the API: example list `{id, split}` only;
  run results and the comparison give held-out totals only; the calls page gives `content: null` + `held_out: true`; a prompt test takes only `dev` examples.
- **Migrations (design §4 "Dữ liệu có sẵn khi P1 migrate", E11, R14):** `<timestamp>_nhom_b_m1_golden_set` (M1-T3: new tables and columns, data move) and
  `<timestamp>_nhom_b_m1_run_items` (M1-T4: drops `items`, `example_ids`), timestamps after `20261029090000`. Existing examples → `split='dev'`, `source='manual'`, `expected`
  NULL; each old `items` element → one `ai_eval_run_items` row with `input` from the example if it still exists, else NULL; old runs `legacy = true`. CHECKs are written by
  hand at the end (invisible to the drift check, as `20261029090000_buoc8_identity/migration.sql:41-47`).
- **Design-fixed copy:** "Chốt phiên bản", "held-out", "1 ví dụ held-out đã bị xem" (as `{count} ví dụ held-out đã bị xem`), "trước khi có phiên bản", "trước đạt, nay trượt".
- **PRs:** **M1-A** = T1–T6: API only, no visible behaviour change (examples without `expected` still accepted, `GET …/runs/:id` still lists every item). **M1-B** (after
  M1-A merged and rebased) = T7–T19: API endpoints, admin screens, mocked and real-stack e2e, smoke. API and admin ship together because `admin-e2e-real` creates an
  example through the admin dialog, which must send `expected` once the API requires it.

**Review focus (M1)** — inputs the design implies but its cases do not name; each has a test in the task shown:
1. `word` with other case or spaces than the catalog lemma (`" Deploy "` vs `deploy`) → the entry is still found for rules and forms (M1-T6).
2. Expected forms typed with other case, a trailing `.` or a curly apostrophe (`Don’t` vs `don't`) → match, because both sides go through `normalizeAnswer` (M1-T6).
3. A held-out id sent in `example_ids` of a prompt test → 400, its output never computed (M1-T11).
4. A migrated example with `expected` NULL inside a run → `expected_result` NULL, counted as `expected_none`, never as a failure (M1-T1, M1-T5).
5. A bulk row whose `expected` cell is plain text (`deploy, deployed`) → only that row is rejected, by line, with `expected: must be an object` (M1-T7, M1-T14).

**Choices this milestone makes where the design leaves room:** 422 `expected-invalid` for B2 on single create/patch, per-row in bulk; the empty working set counts as
0% held-out; PATCH of a hidden held-out example → 409; `GET …/runs/:id` keeps `items` (dev only) for v1 compatibility while the admin reads `GET …/runs/:id/items`; a frozen
set also gets `GET …/sets/:id` (needed to reveal an example deleted from the working set); runs store `price_cached_input` too, so cost stays computed as today (F6).

### M1-T1: Pure rules — types, expected answers, split, run statistics

**Files:** Create `src/ai/eval/eval.types.ts`, `src/ai/eval/expected.ts`, `src/ai/eval/split.ts`, `src/ai/eval/run-stats.ts`, `src/ai/eval/eval-rules.port.ts`, each with a
`.spec.ts` beside it.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `AiOperation`, `AI_VALID_POS`, `LEMMA_MAX`, `WORD_MAX`, `PRIVATE_WORD_VERDICTS` (`src/ai/ai.types.ts`, `src/ai/ai.constants.ts:61-67, 88`);
  `EvalRunStatus` (`src/ai/eval/eval-runs.repository.ts:18`).
- Produces:
  - `eval.types.ts`: contract types `EvalSplit`, `ExampleSource`, `RuleStatus`, `RuleResult`; plus `EVAL_SPLITS`, `EXAMPLE_SOURCES` (const arrays),
    `RunItemStatus = 'pending' | 'succeeded' | 'transient_failure' | 'permanent_failure' | 'unavailable' | 'in_progress' | 'skipped'`, `ExpectedResult = 'pass' | 'fail'`.
  - `expected.ts`: contract types `ExpectedForms`, `ExpectedLookup`, `ExpectedVerdict`; `EXPECTED_OPERATIONS: readonly AiOperation[]` (the four of design §5.1);
    `EXPECTED_FORMS_MAX = 50`; `expectedViolations(op: AiOperation, expected: unknown): string[]`; `normalizeExpected(op: AiOperation, expected: unknown):
    Record<string, unknown> | null` (trim strings, drop duplicate forms keeping the first; call only when there is no violation);
    `expectedResultFor(rules: Pick<EvalRules, 'matchesExpected'> | null, op: AiOperation, output: Record<string, unknown> | null, expected: Record<string, unknown> | null):
    ExpectedResult | null`.
  - `split.ts`: `nextSplit(heldOut: number, total: number, ratio: number): EvalSplit`.
  - `eval-rules.port.ts`: contract type `EvalRules`; `RULE_OPERATIONS = ['generate_cloze', 'generate_meaning_question', 'generate_sentence_mcq'] as const`;
    `ruleResultFor(rules: EvalRules | null, op: AiOperation, input: Record<string, unknown>, output: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<RuleResult>`.
  - `run-stats.ts`: `RunItemResult = { exampleId: string; position: number; split: EvalSplit; status: RunItemStatus; rule: RuleResult | null; expectedResult:
    ExpectedResult | null }`; `SplitStats = { total; pending; skipped; shape_pass; shape_fail; shape_transient; rule_pass; rule_fail; rule_skipped; rule_not_applicable;
    expected_pass; expected_fail; expected_none; failing: number }`; `itemPasses(item: RunItemResult): boolean`; `runStats(items: RunItemResult[]): { dev: SplitStats;
    held_out: SplitStats }`; `isComplete(status: EvalRunStatus, items: Array<Pick<RunItemResult, 'status'>>): boolean`; `RegressionCriterion = 'shape' | 'rule' |
    'expected'`; `compareRuns(a: RunItemResult[], b: RunItemResult[]): { a: ReturnType<typeof runStats>; b: ReturnType<typeof runStats>; regressions: { dev:
    Array<{ exampleId: string; failed: RegressionCriterion[] }>; held_out: number } }`.
  - Definitions pinned by the tests: `shape_pass` = `succeeded`; `shape_fail` = any status other than `succeeded`, `pending`, `skipped`; `shape_transient` ⊂ `shape_fail`;
    `rule_*` and `expected_*` count only `succeeded` items; `expected_none` = `succeeded` with `expectedResult` null; `itemPasses` = `succeeded` ∧ rule ≠ `fail` ∧ expected ≠
    `fail`; `failing` = non-pending, non-skipped items that do not pass; a regression = both items non-pending, non-skipped, `itemPasses(a) ∧ ¬itemPasses(b)`, listed in
    `b`'s position order, `failed` in the order shape, rule, expected.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

```ts
// Local helpers: tenFromEmpty(r) folds nextSplit over 10 additions starting at (heldOut 0, total 0); sevenAfter14Migrated(r) over 7 additions
// starting at (0, 14). rules = { matchesExpected: () => true }; stub = { check: vi.fn(async () => stubResult), formsOf, matchesExpected }.
// split.spec.ts — design §12 "bộ 0, 1, đúng 30%", R21, R14
expect(nextSplit(0, 0, 0.3)).toBe('held_out');            // empty set
expect(nextSplit(0, 1, 0.3)).toBe('held_out');
expect(nextSplit(1, 1, 0.3)).toBe('dev');
expect(nextSplit(3, 10, 0.3)).toBe('dev');                 // exactly 30%
expect(nextSplit(2, 10, 0.3)).toBe('held_out');
expect(tenFromEmpty(0.3)).toEqual(['held_out','dev','dev','dev','held_out','dev','dev','held_out','dev','dev']);
expect(sevenAfter14Migrated(0.3)).toEqual(['held_out','held_out','held_out','held_out','held_out','held_out','dev']);
// expected.spec.ts
expect(expectedViolations('generate_cloze', undefined)).toEqual(['expected: required for generate_cloze']);
expect(expectedViolations('generate_cloze', null)).toEqual(['expected: required for generate_cloze']);
expect(expectedViolations('generate_cloze', 'deploy, deployed')).toEqual(['expected: must be an object']);
expect(expectedViolations('generate_cloze', { forms: [] })).toEqual(['expected.forms: at least one form']);
expect(expectedViolations('generate_cloze', { forms: ['deploy', ' '] })).toEqual(['expected.forms.1: empty']);
expect(expectedViolations('generate_cloze', { forms: Array(51).fill('x') })).toEqual(['expected.forms: at most 50 forms']);
expect(expectedViolations('generate_meaning_question', { forms: ['go'], extra: 1 })).toEqual(['expected: unknown key extra']);
expect(expectedViolations('lookup_word', { lemma: 'run', part_of_speech: 'gerund' })).toEqual(['expected.part_of_speech: not a part of speech']);
expect(expectedViolations('check_private_word', { verdict: 'maybe' })).toEqual(['expected.verdict: ok or not_recognized']);
expect(expectedViolations('translate_sentence', { forms: ['x'] })).toEqual(['expected: translate_sentence has no expected answer']);
expect(expectedViolations('generate_sentence_mcq', null)).toEqual([]);
expect(normalizeExpected('generate_cloze', { forms: [' deploy ', 'deploy', 'deployed'] })).toEqual({ forms: ['deploy', 'deployed'] });
expect(expectedResultFor(null, 'generate_cloze', { accepted_answers: ['go'] }, { forms: ['go'] })).toBeNull();
expect(expectedResultFor({ matchesExpected: () => false }, 'generate_cloze', { accepted_answers: ['x'] }, { forms: ['go'] })).toBe('fail');
expect(expectedResultFor(rules, 'generate_cloze', null, { forms: ['go'] })).toBeNull();          // no output
expect(expectedResultFor(rules, 'generate_cloze', { accepted_answers: ['go'] }, null)).toBeNull(); // migrated example (Review focus 4)
expect(expectedResultFor(null, 'lookup_word', { lemma: 'Run', part_of_speech: 'verb' }, { lemma: ' run', part_of_speech: 'Verb' })).toBe('pass');
expect(expectedResultFor(null, 'lookup_word', { lemma: 'ran', part_of_speech: 'verb' }, { lemma: 'run', part_of_speech: 'verb' })).toBe('fail');
expect(expectedResultFor(null, 'check_private_word', { verdict: 'ok' }, { verdict: 'not_recognized' })).toBe('fail');
// eval-rules.port.spec.ts — B9
expect(await ruleResultFor(null, 'generate_cloze', IN, OUT)).toEqual({ status: 'skipped', problems: [], reason: 'no-rules' });
expect(await ruleResultFor(null, 'lookup_word', IN, OUT)).toEqual({ status: 'not_applicable', problems: [], reason: null });
expect(await ruleResultFor(stub, 'translate_sentence', IN, OUT)).toEqual({ status: 'not_applicable', problems: [], reason: null });
expect(stub.check).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
expect(await ruleResultFor(stub, 'generate_cloze', IN, OUT)).toBe(stubResult); expect(stub.check).toHaveBeenCalledWith('generate_cloze', IN, OUT);
// run-stats.spec.ts — fixture: d1 succeeded/pass/pass, d2 transient_failure, d3 succeeded/fail/pass, d4 succeeded/skipped/null, d5 pending; h1 succeeded/pass/fail
expect(runStats(ITEMS).dev).toEqual({ total: 5, pending: 1, skipped: 0, shape_pass: 3, shape_fail: 1, shape_transient: 1, rule_pass: 1, rule_fail: 1,
  rule_skipped: 1, rule_not_applicable: 0, expected_pass: 2, expected_fail: 0, expected_none: 1, failing: 2 });
expect(runStats(ITEMS).held_out).toEqual({ total: 1, pending: 0, skipped: 0, shape_pass: 1, shape_fail: 0, shape_transient: 0, rule_pass: 1, rule_fail: 0,
  rule_skipped: 0, rule_not_applicable: 0, expected_pass: 0, expected_fail: 1, expected_none: 0, failing: 1 });
expect(isComplete('finished', [{ status: 'succeeded' }, { status: 'transient_failure' }])).toBe(true);   // R11
expect(isComplete('finished', [{ status: 'pending' }])).toBe(false);
expect(isComplete('finished', [{ status: 'skipped' }])).toBe(false);
expect(isComplete('failed', [{ status: 'succeeded' }])).toBe(false);
// a: x1 pass, x2 pass, x3 shape fail, x4 pass, h1 pass, h2 pass; b: x1 rule fail, x2 pass, x3 pass, x4 pending, h1 expected fail, h2 pass
expect(compareRuns(A, B).regressions).toEqual({ dev: [{ exampleId: 'x1', failed: ['rule'] }], held_out: 1 });
```

  cascade, `exampleId` (no FK), `position Int`, `split`, `input Json?`, `expected Json?`, `status String @default("pending")`, `reason String?`, `output Json?`, `rawOutput

  (contract; unused until M3); `@@unique([runId, exampleId])`, `@@index([runId, status])`).

  `ai_eval_set_items_split_check`, `ai_eval_run_items_split_check`, `ai_eval_run_items_status_check` (the seven `RunItemStatus`), `ai_eval_run_items_expected_result_check`
  (`expected_result IS NULL OR expected_result IN ('pass','fail')`).

- [ ] **Step 2: Run** `pnpm --filter api test src/identity/deletion/account-data-map.spec.ts` — FAIL.
- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `'ai_eval_sets.frozen_by': 'keep-id'` in the map. Generate the DDL with `pnpm --filter api exec prisma migrate dev --create-only --name
  nhom_b_m1_golden_set` on a local DB in step with `dev` (or write it by hand like `20261029090000_buoc8_identity`), then append the data move and the CHECKs:

```sql
UPDATE ai_eval_runs SET legacy = true;
UPDATE ai_eval_runs r SET price_input = m.price_input, price_cached_input = m.price_cached_input, price_output = m.price_output
  FROM ai_models m WHERE m.provider = r.provider AND m.model_id = r.model AND r.status IN ('queued', 'running');
INSERT INTO ai_eval_run_items (run_id, example_id, position, split, input, expected, status, reason, output, raw_output, latency_ms, cost_usd)
SELECT r.id, (i->>'example_id')::uuid, coalesce(array_position(r.example_ids, (i->>'example_id')::uuid), 0) - 1, 'dev', e.input, NULL,
       i->>'status', i->>'reason', NULLIF(i->'output', 'null'::jsonb), i->>'raw_output', (i->>'latency_ms')::int, (i->>'cost_usd')::numeric
FROM ai_eval_runs r CROSS JOIN LATERAL jsonb_array_elements(r.items) AS i LEFT JOIN ai_eval_examples e ON e.id = (i->>'example_id')::uuid;
-- ids of a run still queued or running at deploy time that have no result yet
INSERT INTO ai_eval_run_items (run_id, example_id, position, split, input, status, reason)
SELECT r.id, x.id, x.ord - 1, 'dev', e.input, CASE WHEN e.id IS NULL THEN 'skipped' ELSE 'pending' END, CASE WHEN e.id IS NULL THEN 'example-deleted' END
FROM ai_eval_runs r CROSS JOIN LATERAL unnest(r.example_ids) WITH ORDINALITY AS x(id, ord) LEFT JOIN ai_eval_examples e ON e.id = x.id
WHERE r.status IN ('queued', 'running')
  AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM ai_eval_run_items t WHERE t.run_id = r.id AND t.example_id = x.id);
```

**Files:** Create `src/ai/eval/eval-sets.service.ts` (+ spec), `src/ai/eval/held-out.ts` (+ spec); modify `src/ai/eval/eval-examples.service.ts` (+ spec),
`src/ai/api/admin-ai-eval.controller.ts` (+ spec), `src/ai/ai.module.ts`.

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `SetView = { id; operation; version; held_out_ratio: number; frozen_by; frozen_by_user: UserRef; frozen_at; dev_count; held_out_count; revealed_count }`;
    `SetItemView = { example_id; position; split; revealed_at: string | null; in_working_set: boolean; input: unknown | null; expected: unknown | null }` (input and
    expected only for `dev` or revealed items).
  - `EvalSetsService.freeze(actorId: string, op: AiOperation): Promise<SetView>` — one transaction: read the working set; 422 `eval-set-incomplete` with
    `missing_expected` (ids of examples of the four operations with `expected` NULL, in list order) and `no_held_out`; `version` = max + 1; `held_out_ratio` = the
    setting; items copied with `position`, `split`, `input`, `expected`; unique violation (P2002) → 409 `eval-set-conflict`.
  - `EvalSetsService.list(op): Promise<{ items: SetView[] }>` (newest first), `get(id): Promise<SetView & { items: SetItemView[] }>` (404 when unknown),
    `latest(op): Promise<{ id: string; version: number } | null>` (M2's publish gate can reuse it).
  - `EvalExamplesService.reveal(id: string): Promise<RevealedExample>`, `RevealedExample = { id; operation; split: 'dev'; input; expected; note: string | null;
    revealed_at: string; deleted: boolean }`: a held-out example → `split = 'dev'`, `revealed_at = now`, and every `ai_eval_set_items` row of it with `split =
    'held_out' AND revealed_at IS NULL` gets `revealed_at = now`; a `dev` example → 409 `example-not-held-out`; an example gone from the working set → from its latest
    set item (`note` null, `deleted: true`) when one is held-out (409 `example-not-held-out` when all are dev); unknown id → 404.
  - `@Injectable() class HeldOutReader { hiddenIds(exampleIds: string[]): Promise<Set<string>> }` — hidden = example exists with `split = 'held_out'`, or example gone and
    it has a held-out set item and none of its set items has `revealed_at`. M3 reuses it for judge calls.
  - Routes: `POST /v1/admin/ai/eval/sets` (body `{ operation }`, 201), `GET …/sets?operation`, `GET …/sets/:id`, `POST …/examples/:id/reveal` (200).

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing tests**

    (B1); empty set → `missing_expected: []`, `no_held_out: true`; `translate_sentence` with `expected` null and one held-out → freezes; existing max version 2 → created
    `version: 3`, `heldOutRatio: 0.3`, `aiEvalSetItem.createMany` data equals the examples' `position`, `split`, `input`, `expected`; `aiEvalSet.create` throws P2002 →
    409 `eval-set-conflict` (B37); `get` → a held-out item without `revealed_at` has `input: null, expected: null`, a revealed one has its input, a deleted one
    `in_working_set: false`; `list` → `revealed_count: 1` when one held-out item is revealed (B4).
  - `eval-examples.service.spec.ts`: `reveal` of a held-out example → `aiEvalExample.updateMany({ where: { id, split: 'held_out' }, data: { split: 'dev', revealedAt: NOW } })`
    and `aiEvalSetItem.updateMany({ where: { exampleId: id, split: 'held_out', revealedAt: null }, data: { revealedAt: NOW } })`; response has `input`, `deleted: false`;
    a dev example → 409 `example-not-held-out`; gone example with a held-out set item in versions 1 and 2 → input of version 2, `deleted: true`; unknown → 404.
    `update()` of a dev example never calls `aiEvalSetItem` or `aiEvalRunItem` (B5).

    `status <> 'pending'`, position order; `stats` over every item. `get(id).items` = the same dev-only list (held-out never listed, B3).

for `generate_cloze` and `check_private_word` with `PUT /v1/admin/ai/operations/:op` — temporary: M2-T13 drops the model choice and moves `partM1()` onto config drafts) and `async function partM1()` (contract §7);
later milestones add `partM2()` … and call them in order.

Steps of `partM1()` (W1–W4 catalog verbs `zq<6 letters>` with `past:<w>ed; third_person:<w>s`, N a word not in the catalog):
1. Forms: `GET …/examples/forms?word=W1&part_of_speech=verb` → sorted `[W1, W1ed, W1s]`; `unspecified` → the same; `word=N` → `null`.
2. B2: create without `expected` → 422 `expected-invalid`. Create W1, W2, N, W3, W4 with expected forms → splits `held_out, dev, dev, dev, held_out` (list shows `{id, split}`
   only for W1, W4).
3. B1: a psql row `ai_eval_examples` (cloze, dev, `expected` NULL) → `POST …/sets` 422 `eval-set-incomplete` with its id; delete it; freeze → 201 version 1, `dev_count 3`,
   `held_out_count 2`. B37: psql in the background (`execFile`) runs `BEGIN; INSERT INTO ai_eval_sets (operation, version, held_out_ratio, frozen_by) VALUES
   ('generate_cloze', 2, 0.3, '<admin id>'); SELECT pg_sleep(3); COMMIT;`, after 0.5 s `POST …/sets` → 409 `eval-set-conflict`; delete that row with psql; then two parallel
   `POST …/sets` → each 201 or 409, versions unique.
4. Runs with `fake:ok`: on version 1 and on the working set → `finished`, `complete: true` (version) ; `…/items` of the version run → dev items W2, N, W3: W2/W3 rule `pass`,
   N rule `skipped` / `not-in-catalog` (B8), all expected `pass`; `stats.held_out.total: 2`; `GET …/runs/:id` `items` has no W1/W4 (B3).
5. `fake:bad-form` on version 1 → W2/W3 rule `fail` with `unknown-form:<w>ment`, expected `fail`; compare (ok run, bad run) → dev regressions W2, N, W3, `held_out: 2`;
   compare (version run, working-set run) → 409 `eval-set-mismatch` (B10).
6. B6: calls of `eval-<okRun>-<W2>` → content present, `held_out: false`; of `eval-<okRun>-<W1>` → `content: null`, `held_out: true`.
7. B7: `POST …/prompts/generate_cloze/test` without `example_ids` → 3 items, inputs W2, N, W3; with `example_ids: [W1]` → 400.
8. B4: reveal W1 → 200 with its input; list shows W1 `dev` with `revealed_at`; version 1 `revealed_count: 1`; calls of `eval-<okRun>-<W1>` now carry content; reveal W1
   again → 409 `example-not-held-out`; delete W4 then reveal W4 → 200, `deleted: true`, `revealed_count: 2`.
9. B5: PATCH W2 `expected` to `{ forms: [W2] }` → `GET …/sets/:id` item W2 and the ok run's item W2 keep the old forms.
10. `check_private_word` example (expected `{ verdict: 'ok' }`) added through `…/examples/bulk` with `split: 'dev'` (one example alone would land in held-out), run on
    the working set → rule `not_applicable`, expected `pass` (B9).

## Milestone M2 — Config lifecycle (design K3)

**Goal:** prompt + model + effort + thinking + tokens of an AI operation become one versioned config: a draft kept on the server → test and eval → publish through
a warning gate → rollback; the gateway reads the live config; the two save `PUT`s go away. New folder `src/ai/configs/` and controller
`src/ai/api/admin-ai-configs.controller.ts`; three tables (`ai_configs`, the pointer `ai_live_configs`, `ai_config_events`); eval runs record `config_id` +
`config_version`; the few-shot check (T2) reaches the Practice rules only through M1's `EvalRules` port; the admin merges Thao tác and Prompt into one page "Cấu hình"
(`/ai/configs`). The switch-over (copy migration, gateway, `PUT` removal, scripts, e2e) lands in one PR so `admin-e2e-real` never runs against a half state. Review:
R1, R2, R4, R11–R13, R15. M1 rewrites `src/ai/eval/**` first: read those files again after M1 merged.

**The two gate exceptions (D37, D38; design §6.3 step 2, §6.3b).** A live config **without a model** needs no baseline: `baselineRequirement` (M2-T2) answers
`'not-required'`, so no baseline run and no comparison warning, while the draft still needs its complete run on the newest frozen set (B29); this is how every operation
gets its first model on a fresh DB. A draft **without a model** publishes with no run at all ("turn off", M2-T5, B48): `expected_version` and the pointer's version
are still checked and an `ai_config_events` row is written; the admin confirms in a dialog (M2-T7); a rollback turns the operation back on. The scripts' helper
(M2-T10) reads `publish-check`, runs a baseline only when `baseline.required` is true and none exists, and sends `acknowledge_warnings` whenever warnings exist.

**Constraints (M2):**
- **`/v1` exception** (design §2 "Quyết định cũ bị đổi", §6.6): `PUT /v1/admin/ai/prompts/:op` and `PUT /v1/admin/ai/operations/:op` → 404. `GET /v1/admin/ai/operations`,
  `GET …/prompts/:op`, `GET …/prompts/:op/revisions`, `POST …/prompts/:op/test`, `GET …/status` and the model views keep their shapes; `POST …/eval/runs` keeps `prompt`
  and adds `config`.
- **New endpoints** (design §9 row P3): `@RequirePermission('settings.manage')`; writes `@RateLimit('user.write')`; `POST …/draft/test` hits `ai.test` only after its own
  checks and has no Idempotency-Key (as `src/ai/api/admin-ai-prompts.controller.ts:47-55`); `POST …/eval/runs` stays `@Idempotent()`.
- **Problem types** exactly as contract §6 (P3 rows): `run-is-evidence`, `draft-exists`, `draft-busy`, `draft-version-conflict`, `eval-required`, `baseline-required`,
  `publish-stale`, `publish-warnings`, `no-previous`, `model-in-use`, `few-shot-invalid`; reused `not-found` 404, `validation-failed` 400, `model-not-set` 409,
  `provider-not-configured` 409; M2-only run error `config-changed`.
- **Zero behaviour change at the switch-over** (M2-T8): every operation keeps its prompt text, model, effort, thinking, tokens and its `prompt_revision` number (config
  `number` = current `ai_prompts.revision`, design §6.1).
- **Numbers:** a new draft takes `number = ai_live_configs.last_number + 1` (never handed out twice, even after a draft is deleted); a config's `version` starts at 0 and
  +1 on each save; `ai_live_configs.version` +1 on each publish or rollback.
- **Gate** (design §6.3, §5.3): *S* = the operation's frozen set with the highest `version`; "xong trọn" = M1's `isComplete` (run `finished`, no item `pending` or
  `skipped`); "lần gần nhất" = newest `created_at`, then `id`; a warning when a criterion's pass rate over all items of *S* drops by **more than** 5 points (integer
  arithmetic; exactly 5 → none), when a dev example passed before and fails now, or when the held-out failure count rises. Warnings never block. Live config without
  a model → no baseline, no warning (D37); draft without a model → no run, no warning, steps 4–5 only (D38).
- **Models in use (D40, B49, B50):** a model named by any `ai_configs` row (draft, live, previous or `retired`) is never deleted (409 `model-in-use`, FK RESTRICT);
  the model select of "Cấu hình" hides models that are `hidden` (admin flag, M2-T1) or `lifecycle = 'retired'`, except the draft's own (M2-T6).
- **Races:** FK RESTRICT, the partial unique index, the CHECKs and the two races (B24, B31) are proven in `partM2()` with two parallel requests (E19); no new
  Testcontainers command.
- The vi copy quoted in M2-T6 and M2-T7 is chosen by this plan (the design fixes none for these screens).
- **PRs:** **M2-a** = T1–T5 (API core, dormant: the new tables stay empty and nothing calls them); **M2-b** = T6–T7 (admin page at `/ai/configs`, not in the menu yet,
  mocked tests); **M2-c** = T8–T13 (switch-over: gateway, `PUT`s, scripts, menu, e2e, smoke). M2-a and M2-b leave `admin-e2e-real` untouched (gateway, `PUT`s and
  `prompt: 'current'` change only in T8–T9).

**Review focus items (M2)**, each pinned by a test in the task named:
1. A draft saved again while its eval run is still queued: the job must not run the newer text under the older `config_version` → the run ends
   `failed`, `error = 'config-changed'`, no provider call (M2-T4).
2. Rollback pressed on a stale page after another admin published: it must not roll back the newer config → 409 `publish-stale` through
   `expected_live_version` (M2-T5).
3. A draft deleted, then a new draft: its `number` must not repeat the deleted one, which test and eval calls in `ai_calls.prompt_revision` already
   carry (M2-T3).
4. An old client sending `prompt: 'current'` after the switch-over gets a run of the live config (with `config_id`, `config_version`), usable as the
   gate's baseline (M2-T8).
5. The seeded few-shot examples (`src/ai/prompts/initial-prompts.ts`) must pass T2 in a catalog that holds their word (`deploy` is in
   `scripts/fixtures/learner-sample.csv`), or every scripted draft save fails 422 (M2-T10 runs `learner:setup` on a DB with that sample).

### M2-T2: Gate rules as pure functions (PR M2-a)

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/api/src/ai/configs/publish-check.ts`, `apps/api/src/ai/configs/publish-check.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (M1-T1, `src/ai/eval/run-stats.ts` as the M1 plan names them; if M1 merged other names, use M1's): `RunItemResult`, `itemPasses`,
  `runStats` (→ `{ dev: SplitStats; held_out: SplitStats }`), `isComplete(status, items)` ("xong trọn", design §5.3), `compareRuns(a, b)` (→
  `regressions.dev`, `regressions.held_out`). M2 does not define a second "xong trọn" or a second pass rate.
- Produces:
  - `type Tally = { passed: number; counted: number }`; `gateTallies(items: RunItemResult[]): { shape: Tally; rule: Tally; expected: Tally }` — sums of
    `runStats` over dev **and** held-out: shape = `shape_pass` of `shape_pass + shape_fail`; rule = `rule_pass` of `rule_pass + rule_fail`;
    expected = `expected_pass` of `expected_pass + expected_fail`.
  - `type PublishWarning = { code: 'rate-drop'; criterion: 'shape' | 'rule' | 'expected'; before: Tally; after: Tally } | { code: 'dev-regressed';
    example_ids: string[] } | { code: 'held-out-failures-up'; before: number; after: number }` (in `configs.types.ts`; M3 adds judge criteria).
  - `publishWarnings(candidate: RunItemResult[], baseline: RunItemResult[]): PublishWarning[]` — order: `rate-drop` (shape, rule, expected), then
    `dev-regressed` (= `compareRuns(baseline, candidate).regressions.dev` ids, when not empty), then `held-out-failures-up` (`runStats(...).held_out.failing`
    of baseline vs candidate, when it rose); a criterion with `counted = 0` on either side is not compared; held-out ids are never listed.
  - `baselineRequirement(live: { modelRef: string | null }): 'required' | 'not-required'` — `'required'` when `modelRef !== null`; `'not-required'` when it is
    `null` (D37: a live config without a model cannot be evaluated, so there is nothing to compare with).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** (`publish-check.spec.ts`):
  - `gateTallies` on `[succeeded/rule pass/expected pass, succeeded/rule skipped/expected null, transient_failure]` → `{ shape: { passed: 2, counted: 3 },
    rule: { passed: 1, counted: 1 }, expected: { passed: 1, counted: 1 } }`.
  - Rate edge: baseline 20 held-out-free items all passing; candidate with one shape failure → no `rate-drop` (exactly 5 points); with two → the first
    warning is `{ code: 'rate-drop', criterion: 'shape', before: { passed: 20, counted: 20 }, after: { passed: 18, counted: 20 } }`.
  - Rule all `skipped` in the candidate → no rule warning.
  - Dev `e1` passes in the baseline and fails in the candidate → contains `{ code: 'dev-regressed', example_ids: ['e1'] }`; held-out `h1` the same way →
    contains `{ code: 'held-out-failures-up', before: 0, after: 1 }` and no warning names `h1`.
  - Identical item lists → `[]`.
  - `baselineRequirement({ modelRef: '0b3c…' })` → `'required'`; `baselineRequirement({ modelRef: null })` → `'not-required'` (D37).
- [ ] **Step 2: Run** `pnpm test -- src/ai/configs/publish-check.spec.ts` → FAIL (module not found).
- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** the signatures above. The 5-point rule in integers, `a` = candidate, `b` = baseline:
  `100 * (b.passed * a.counted - a.passed * b.counted) > 5 * a.counted * b.counted`.
- [ ] **Step 4: Run** the same command → PASS.
- [ ] **Step 5: Commit** `feat(api): add the publish gate rules of ai configs`.

  (`src/ai/models/model-rules.ts:17`); `toSelection` (`src/ai/runner/selection.ts:40`); `RunnerService.execute` (`src/ai/runner/runner.service.ts:84`);
  `TestItem` (`src/ai/prompts/prompts.service.ts:51`); the cost estimate of `OperationSettingsService.item` (`src/ai/models/operation-settings.service.ts:118-141`,
  moved into a pure `operationEstimate(model, maxOutputTokens, usdVndRate)` in `src/ai/models/operation-estimate.ts`); the `EvalRules` port registered
  through `AiService.registerEvalRules`, read back with `EvalRulesRegistry.get()` and checked with `ruleResultFor(rules, op, input, output)` (M1-T5, M1-T1).
- Produces:
  - `ConfigView = { id; operation; number; version; status: ConfigStatus; instructions; examples: PromptExample[]; model: { id; provider; model_id } | null;
    effort; thinking: 'off' | null; max_output_tokens; note; created_from; created_by_user: UserRef; created_at; published_by_user: UserRef | null;
    published_at; estimate: CostEstimate | null }`;
    `ConfigsView = { operation; input_fields: string[]; guard: string; live_version: number; live: ConfigView; draft: ConfigView | null;
    previous: ConfigView | null }` (in `configs.types.ts`).
  - `ConfigsService.get(op): Promise<ConfigsView>` — 404 `not-found` when the operation has no `ai_live_configs` row (only before M2-T8).
  - `createDraft(actorId, op, body: { from_config_id?: string }): Promise<ConfigView>` — default the live config; `from_config_id` must be a `published` or
    `retired` config of `op`, else 400 `validation-failed` (`from_config_id: …`); copies instructions, examples, model, effort, thinking, tokens;
    `created_from` = source; `version` 0; `number` from `UPDATE ai_live_configs SET last_number = last_number + 1 … RETURNING` in the same transaction;
    a draft already there (read, or P2002 of the partial unique) → 409 `draft-exists` with `extensions.draft: ConfigView` (B23).
  - `saveDraft(actorId, op, body: SaveDraftBody): Promise<ConfigView>` with `saveDraftSchema = z.strictObject({ expected_version: int ≥ 0, instructions:
    string, examples: unknown[], model_ref: uuid (lower-cased) | null, effort: string(1..20) | null, thinking: 'off' | null, max_output_tokens: int | null,
    note: trimmed string ≤ CONFIG_NOTE_MAX | null })`. Order: no draft → 404; version ≠ `expected_version` → 409 `draft-version-conflict`;
    `promptViolations` → 400; unknown model → 400 `model_ref: not found`; provider not registered → 409 `provider-not-configured`;
    `selectionViolations` → 400; then T2 (design §6.2): each few-shot `{input, output}` through `ruleResultFor(registry.get(), op, input, output)` — `fail` → 422
    `few-shot-invalid`, `extensions.examples: [{ index, problems }]` for the failing ones only, nothing written; `skipped`, `not_applicable` or no port
    registered → accepted; then `updateMany where { id, status: 'draft', version: expected_version }` with `version: { increment: 1 }`, 0 rows → 409
    `draft-version-conflict`.
  - `deleteDraft(op): Promise<void>` — no draft → 404; a run with `config_id` = draft and status `queued` / `running` → 409 `draft-busy` (B26); else delete.
  - `testDraft(actorId, op, body: { example_ids?: string[] ≤ TEST_EXAMPLES_MAX }): Promise<{ items: TestItem[] }>` — no draft → 404; no model → 409
    `model-not-set`; provider missing → 409 `provider-not-configured`; inputs by M1's dev-only rule (B7); `ai.test` hit after those checks; each input
    `runner.execute({ purpose: 'test', …, selection: toSelection(op, draft, model), prompt: { instructions, examples, revision: draft.number } })`.
  - Routes under `@Controller('admin/ai/configs')`: `GET :op`, `POST :op/draft` (201), `PUT :op/draft`, `DELETE :op/draft` (204), `POST :op/draft/test` (200).

### M2-T4: Eval runs of a config (PR M2-a)

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/api/src/ai/eval/eval-runs.service.ts` (`createRunSchema`, `create`, run views, `remove`), `apps/api/src/ai/eval/eval-job.ts` (prompt and

  - Body: M1's fields plus `config?: 'live' | 'draft'`; exactly one of `prompt` and `config` (else 400). `config` → the live config or the draft (404
    `not-found` when absent; 409 `model-not-set` without a model); the row stores `configId`, `configVersion`, the resolved selection (as today,
    `eval-runs.service.ts:91-96`), `promptRevision` and `draft` NULL. `prompt` keeps its M1 meaning in this PR (`'current'` changes in M2-T8).
  - Job: a run with `configId` loads that `ai_configs` row once before its pool; row gone → `fail(run, 'config-deleted')`; `version ≠ configVersion` →
    `fail(run, 'config-changed')`; else prompt `{ instructions, examples, revision: config.number }` (so `ai_calls.prompt_revision` = the config number,
    design §6.5).
  - Run views (list, get) add `config_id`, `config_version`, `config_number` (`null` when the config row is gone).
  - `remove(id)`: a run named by `ai_config_events.candidate_run_id` or `baseline_run_id` → 409 `run-is-evidence` (B33), checked before the delete; a
    P2003 from the FK maps to the same 409.

  'config-changed'` and the runner was never called; config row gone → `error: 'config-deleted'`; versions equal → the runner got `prompt.revision` = the
  config number. `remove` of a run named by an event → 409 `run-is-evidence`; the view of a run of a deleted draft → `config_number: null`.

### M2-T5: Publish check, publish (with turning an operation off), rollback, history (PR M2-a)

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/api/src/ai/configs/publish.service.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`)
- Modify: `apps/api/src/ai/api/admin-ai-configs.controller.ts` (+ spec), `apps/api/src/ai/ai.module.ts`, `apps/api/src/ai/configs/configs.types.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `publishWarnings`, `baselineRequirement` (M2-T2); M1's `isComplete` and the items as `RunItemResult[]` (M1-T1, M1-T4); `ConfigsService.get` (M2-T3); M1's `ai_eval_sets`
  (`operation`, `version`) and `ai_eval_run_items` (contract §3).
- Produces:
  - `PublishCheckView = { operation; draft: { id; number; version }; turn_off: boolean; set: { id; version } | null; candidate_run_id: string | null; baseline: {
    required: boolean; run_id: string | null }; blockers: Array<'eval-required' | 'baseline-required'>; warnings: PublishWarning[] }`.
  - `PublishService.check(op): Promise<PublishCheckView>` — no draft → 404. **Draft without a model** (D38, design §6.3b) → `turn_off: true`, `candidate_run_id:
    null`, `baseline: { required: false, run_id: null }`, `blockers: []`, `warnings: []`, and no run is read. Otherwise `turn_off: false`; *S* as in the Global
    constraints (none → `set: null`, blocker `eval-required`). Candidate = newest complete run with `config_id` = draft, `config_version` = draft version, `set_id` =
    *S*. Baseline: `baselineRequirement(live)`; when required, newest complete run of the live config at its version on *S*, none → blocker `baseline-required`; when
    not required (live without a model, D37) → `{ required: false, run_id: null }` and no baseline read. Warnings only when both runs exist.
  - `publish(actorId, op, body: { expected_version: number; candidate_run_id: string | null; baseline_run_id: string | null; acknowledge_warnings: boolean })
    : Promise<ConfigsView>` — order: no draft → 409 `publish-stale` (the body names a draft that is gone: published by a parallel request, or deleted; `check` keeps
    404, design §6.3 step 4); `expected_version` ≠ draft version → 409 `draft-version-conflict`; **draft without a model:** either id not null → 409
    `publish-stale`, then straight to the transaction (design §6.3b: steps 1–3 skipped, 4–5 kept); **draft with a model:** no candidate → 409
    `eval-required`; baseline required and none → 409 `baseline-required`; either id ≠ `check()` → 409 `publish-stale`; warnings and
    `acknowledge_warnings` false → 409 `publish-warnings` with `extensions.warnings`. Then one transaction: `updateMany ai_live_configs where { operation,
    version: <read> }` → `{ configId: C, previousConfigId: L, version + 1 }`, 0 rows → 409 `publish-stale` (B31); draft → `published` with
    `publishedBy`, `publishedAt` (guarded by `status: 'draft'` and the version, 0 rows → 409 `publish-stale`); L → `retired`; event `{ kind: 'publish',
    configId: C, fromConfigId: L, candidateRunId, baselineRunId, warnings: <acknowledged>, actorId }` (a turn-off: both run ids NULL, `warnings: []`; the `kind`
    stays `publish`, design §4).
  - `rollback(actorId, op, body: { expected_live_version: number }): Promise<ConfigsView>` — `previous_config_id` NULL → 409 `no-previous` (B32);
    `expected_live_version` ≠ pointer version → 409 `publish-stale`; one transaction: live → `retired`, previous → `published`, pointer `{ configId:
    previous, previousConfigId: null, version + 1 }` guarded by the version; event `{ kind: 'rollback', configId: previous, fromConfigId: live }`; the
    draft is untouched (design §6.4).
  - `history(op, pageSize, pageToken?)` → `{ items: Array<{ id; kind; at; actor_user: UserRef; config: { id; number; model_id: string | null };
    from_config: { id; number; model_id: string | null } | null; candidate_run_id; baseline_run_id; warnings: PublishWarning[] }>; next_page_token }`,
    newest first, bound page token as `eval-runs.service.ts:103-127`.
  - Routes: `GET :op/publish-check`, `POST :op/publish` (200), `POST :op/rollback` (200), `GET :op/history?page_size&page_token`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** (`publish.service.spec.ts`):
  - `check`: no frozen set → `{ set: null, candidate_run_id: null, blockers: ['eval-required'] }`; a complete run of the draft at version 1 while the
    draft is at version 2 → `blockers` contains `eval-required` (B25); only a `failed` run or a `finished` run with a `skipped` item → `eval-required`
    (B27); live with a model and no complete run on *S* → `baseline: { required: true, run_id: null }`, `blockers: ['baseline-required']` (B28); two runs
    of the draft → the newer id; runs on an older set ignored; both runs present → `warnings` = `publishWarnings(...)` of their items.
  - `publish`: each 409 above in the stated order, with its type (B27, B28, B30; `extensions.warnings` equals the check's list); no draft row (a parallel request
    just published it) → 409 `publish-stale`, nothing written (B31, never 404); the pointer
    `updateMany` returning 0 → 409 `publish-stale` and no event written (B31); success → the statements run in the order pointer, draft, live, event; the
    event carries both run ids and the acknowledged warnings; the returned view has `draft: null`, `previous.id` = L.
  - B29 (D37): live config without a model, the draft on `fake:ok` with a complete run R on *S* → `check` gives `{ turn_off: false, candidate_run_id: R, baseline:
    { required: false, run_id: null }, blockers: [], warnings: [] }` and no query for a live run; `publish({ expected_version, candidate_run_id: R, baseline_run_id:
    null, acknowledge_warnings: false })` → 200, event `baselineRunId: null`, `warnings: []`; the same draft without a complete run on *S* → 409 `eval-required`
    (step 1 still applies); a `baseline_run_id` that is not null → 409 `publish-stale`.

    baselineRunId: null, warnings: [] }`, the view's `live.model` null and `previous.model.model_id` `'fake:ok'`; `expected_version: 0` → 409
    `draft-version-conflict`; a `candidate_run_id` → 409 `publish-stale`; the pointer `updateMany` returning 0 → 409 `publish-stale` (B31 holds for a turn-off too);
    `rollback({ expected_live_version: <after> })` → the `fake:ok` config is live again.
  - `rollback`: no previous → 409 `no-previous`; Review Focus 2: `expected_live_version: 3` while the pointer is at 4 → 409 `publish-stale` and nothing
    written; success → previous `published`, live `retired`, pointer `previousConfigId: null`, `version: 5`, the draft row not touched, one `rollback`
    event.
  - Controller: an editor gets 403 on the four routes; `POST …/publish` without the `candidate_run_id` key → 400; with `candidate_run_id: null` → accepted.

### M2-T8: Switch the gateway to the live config (PR M2-c)

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/api/prisma/migrations/<timestamp>_nhom_b_m2_live_configs/migration.sql`, `apps/api/src/ai/configs/live-config.reader.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`)
- Modify: `apps/api/prisma/models/ai.prisma` (drop `AiOperationSetting.model` `:50` and `AiModel.operationSettings` `:30`; comments on `AiPrompt`,
  `AiOperationSetting`: "history only since M2"), `apps/api/src/ai/runner/selection.ts:56-66`, `apps/api/src/ai/runner/readiness.service.ts:9-39`,
  `apps/api/src/ai/runner/runner.service.ts:129-137, 188-193`, `apps/api/src/ai/prompts/prompts.repository.ts:14-17`,
  `apps/api/src/ai/eval/eval-runs.service.ts` (`prompt: 'current'`), `apps/api/src/ai/ai.service.ts`, `apps/api/src/ai/ai.types.ts:27` (comment),
  `apps/api/src/ai/ai.module.ts`, and their specs

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `toSelection` (`selection.ts:40`); the tables of M2-T1.
- Produces:
  - Migration: for each `ai_prompts` row joined to `ai_operation_settings`, insert one `ai_configs` row `{ number: p.revision, version: 0, instructions,
    examples, model_ref, effort, thinking, max_output_tokens, status: 'published', created_by: p.updated_by, created_at: p.updated_at, published_by:
    COALESCE(s.updated_by, p.updated_by), published_at: GREATEST(p.updated_at, s.updated_at) }` and one `ai_live_configs` row `{ config_id, NULL, version
    0, last_number: p.revision }`; `ALTER TABLE ai_operation_settings DROP CONSTRAINT ai_operation_settings_model_ref_fkey` (design §4).
  - `type LiveConfig = { configId: string; number: number; prompt: { instructions: string; examples: PromptExample[]; revision: number }; selection:
    Selection | null }`; `LiveConfigReader.load(op): Promise<LiveConfig>` (one query: pointer → config → model; read on every call, no cache, F3).
  - `SelectionReader.load(op)` = `(await reader.load(op)).selection` (same signature). `ReadinessCheck` ready branch becomes `{ ready: true; selection;
    prompt }` from one `load`; the learner path uses `ready.prompt` (`runner.service.ts:137`), the admin path loads both once when not given
    (`:188-193`); `PromptsRepository.current(op)` reads the live config (`revision` = number); `atRevision` stays for runs made before M2.
  - `POST …/eval/runs` with `prompt: 'current'` = `config: 'live'` (Review Focus 4); `prompt: {instructions, examples}` stays a legacy run (stored
    `draft`, no config, never usable for publish).
  - `AiService.liveConfigNumber(op): Promise<{ number: number; model: string | null } | null>` (contract §4; `model` = the model's `model_id`).
  - Not touched: `src/practice/ai/unseen-questions.ts` (D25, D34, B35): a question made with config N is still served after config N+1 is published and
    keeps `ai_meta.prompt_revision` N (`smoke:buoc-7` AC2 covers the reuse; the runner test below covers the number).

  modelId: 'fake:ok', thinking: 'disabled', … } }`; no model → `selection: null`. Readiness: one `load` per check and the ready result carries its
  prompt. Runner (learner): the ledger records `promptRevision: 7`, `meta.prompt_revision` is 7 and the adapter gets

### M2-T9: Old endpoints read the live config; model guards; the two `PUT`s go (PR M2-c)

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/api/src/ai/api/admin-ai-prompts.controller.ts:29-37`, `apps/api/src/ai/api/admin-ai-operations.controller.ts:22-30`,
  `apps/api/src/ai/prompts/prompts.service.ts` (`get` `:93-101`; delete `save`, `savePromptSchema`), `apps/api/src/ai/models/operation-settings.service.ts`
  (`list` `:66-72`; delete `put`, `putOperationSchema`), `apps/api/src/ai/reports/reports.service.ts:95-133`, `apps/api/src/ai/models/models.service.ts`
  (`update` `:118-133`, `remove` `:153-164`, `views` `:183-188`), and their specs

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `LiveConfigReader` (M2-T8), `ConfigsView`.
- Produces:
  - `GET /v1/admin/ai/prompts/:op` keeps `PromptView`: `revision` = live `number`, `updated_at` / `updated_by` = `published_at` / `published_by`.
  - `GET /v1/admin/ai/operations` keeps `OperationItem`; `version` = `ai_live_configs.version`.
  - Status (`ReportsService.status`) and `ModelView.used_by` read live configs (a draft's model is not "used by").
  - `ModelsService.remove(id)`: any `ai_configs` row with this `model_ref` → 409 `model-in-use` (D40, B49: a `retired` config blocks too, as the FK RESTRICT does;
    M3 adds judge revisions, design §6.1). A model nobody wants to see is hidden from the pickers instead (M2-T6).
  - `ModelsService.update(id, patch)`: `price_input` or `price_output` set to `null` while the model is in the draft or the live config → 409
    `model-in-use` (design §6.1, B34).
  - `PUT …/prompts/:op`, `PUT …/operations/:op` → 404.

  config; `used_by` of a model only in the draft → `[]`; `remove` of a model in the draft, the live, the previous or only a retired config → 409
  `model-in-use` (B34, B49); of an unused model → deleted; `update({ price_input: null })` on the live model → 409, on a model only in a retired config → 200;

### M2-T10: The scripts publish through the real path (PR M2-c)

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/api/scripts/ai-config-publish.mjs`
- Modify: `apps/api/scripts/smoke-buoc-6.mjs` (`choose` `:231-237`; B6#2 `:276-278`; B6#14 `:388-398`; B6#15 `:400-409`; `:421`; B6#25 `:495-510`; steps
  that count `generate_cloze` examples or runs `:424-442`, `:576-596`), `apps/api/scripts/smoke-buoc-7.mjs` (`choose` `:333-341` and its call sites;
  B7E117 `:1119-1135`), `apps/api/scripts/learner-test-setup.mjs:317-338`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the API of M2-T3–T5, T8; M1's `POST …/eval/examples` (with `expected`), `GET …/eval/sets?operation`, `POST …/eval/sets`.
- Produces (`ai-config-publish.mjs`; M2-T12 ports `ensureFrozenSet`, `publishModel`, `rollbackConfig` to the real-stack e2e):
  - `call(method, path, { body?, idem? }) → Promise<{ status, json, text }>` — each script passes its own `api` bound to a token.
  - `ensureFrozenSet(call, op) → { id, version }` — the newest set if one exists; else one example `{ operation, input, note: 'scripted', expected }` per few-shot of the
    operation's seeded prompt in `src/ai/prompts/initial-prompts.ts` (two; three for `check_private_word`), `expected` from that few-shot's `output` (design §5.1:
    `{ forms: accepted_answers }`, `{ forms: [word_in_sentence] }`, `{ lemma, part_of_speech }`, `{ verdict }`, none for the other two; on an empty working set the first
    lands in held-out and the second in dev, which is all a freeze needs), then `POST …/eval/sets`. A 422 `eval-set-incomplete` (older examples without `expected`, as the
    14 migrated B1 examples of a local DB) → throw "`<op>`: examples without an expected answer: `<ids>`. Add it on Bộ ví dụ or delete them, then run again." It never
    edits existing examples; it is meant for a fresh or a set-up DB.
  - `publishModel(call, { op, modelId, maxOutputTokens = null }) → ConfigView` (the new live config): add `fake` model if missing; return at once when
    the live config already has that model and token limit; `ensureFrozenSet`; a leftover draft whose `note` starts with `scripted:` is deleted, any
    other draft → throw; create a draft, save it with the model and `note: 'scripted: <modelId>'`; run `{ set_id, config: 'draft' }` and poll until
    `finished` (≤ 120 s; `failed` → throw); read `publish-check`; `baseline-required` → run `config: 'live'` the same way and read it again; any blocker
    left → throw with the check; `POST …/publish` with `acknowledge_warnings: warnings.length > 0`.
  - `rollbackConfig(call, op) → ConfigView` — `POST …/rollback` with the live `live_version`.
  - `turnOff(call, op) → ConfigView` (D38, the new live config): a leftover `scripted:` draft is deleted, any other draft → throw; create a draft, save it with
    `model_ref: null`, `effort: null`, `thinking: null`, `max_output_tokens: null`, `note: 'scripted: off'`; read `publish-check` (must say `turn_off: true` with no
    blocker, else throw with the check); `POST …/publish` `{ expected_version, candidate_run_id: null, baseline_run_id: null, acknowledge_warnings: false }`. No run.
  - Callers: `smoke-buoc-6` and `smoke-buoc-7` publish through a second admin made with `POST /v1/admin/users` (`staff_role: 'admin'`, schema
    `src/identity/staff/staff.schemas.ts:33`, as the editor at `smoke-buoc-6.mjs:207-209`), so neither admin passes the `ai.test` budget and no registration is spent.
    B6#2 asserts `meta.prompt_revision` = the number `publishModel` returned. B6#14 → the draft saved twice with the same `expected_version` → 200 then 409
    `draft-version-conflict`, `GET …/prompts/generate_cloze/revisions` unchanged. B6#15 → a draft save with a wrong-shape few-shot → 400 with the same violation as
    before. B6#25 → the editor gets 403 on the nine config routes and the admin gets 404 on the two old `PUT`s. A `finally` that returns to `fake:ok` right after one
    publish uses `rollbackConfig`; any other switch uses `publishModel`. B7E117's `choose(op, null)` becomes
    `turnOff(call, 'generate_meaning_question')` (D38) and its `finally` re-enables with `rollbackConfig` (D38: a rollback turns the operation back on).
    `learner:setup` calls `publishModel` per operation with its own admin (a run on a set-up DB changes nothing).

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing run:** API and worker from M2-T9 on a fresh `english_learning_smoke_b6` (`pnpm build && pnpm run:dev`, `AI_FAKE_ENABLED=true`, no
  provider key, `JOB_QUEUE_PREFIX=wordmet-b6-smoke`), then `pnpm smoke:buoc-6` → exits non-zero at "choose fake:ok for generate_cloze" with 404.

  on a fresh DB → exit 0 and `GET /v1/admin/ai/status` shows six operations ready (Review Focus 5: the sample holds `deploy`), then the same command again
  → exit 0 with no new config (`GET …/configs/generate_cloze` number unchanged).

  3. B23: a second `POST …/draft` → 409 `draft-exists`, `draft.id` = the first. B36: a save whose few-shot answer is not a form of a catalog word →

  4. B24 (E19): two `PUT …/draft` at once with the same `expected_version` → statuses `[200, 409]`, the 409 `draft-version-conflict`.
  5. B26: the draft on `fake:slow`, a run started, `DELETE …/draft` while it is `queued` / `running` → 409 `draft-busy`; cancel, delete → 204.
  6. B27, B25: a new draft on `fake:two-blanks`; publish without a run → 409 `eval-required`; eval it, save it once more → `publish-check` blockers
     `['eval-required']`; eval again.
  7. B28: add one example, freeze *S2*, eval the draft on *S2* → `baseline-required`, publish → 409 `baseline-required`; run the live config on *S2* →

  8. B30: publish without `acknowledge_warnings` → 409 `publish-warnings` with the warnings; with a wrong `baseline_run_id` → 409 `publish-stale`.
  9. B31 (E19): two identical acknowledged publishes at once → `[200, 409]`, the 409 `publish-stale` (M2-T5: a request that finds no draft is stale too); `psql`:

  10. B33: `DELETE` the candidate run → 409 `run-is-evidence`. B34: `DELETE` model `fake:two-blanks` (live) and `fake:ok` (previous) → 409

      `no-previous`; an `ai-fake-call` learner call → `prompt_revision` = the rolled-back number; `GET …/history` lists publish, publish, rollback.

      the newest `ai_config_events` row is `kind 'publish'` with `candidate_run_id` and `baseline_run_id` NULL; `rollbackConfig` → live = the `fake:ok` config again
      (later parts rely on it) and an `ai-fake-call` succeeds.

## Milestone M3 — Judge (design K2)

**Goal:** an admin-defined judge (Anthropic model only, blind to who wrote the output) scores eval outputs per criterion; people label outputs and see how well each
judge revision agrees with them; admin AI work gets its own daily USD cap while the learner call cap counts learners only. New folder `src/ai/judge/` (types, pure rules,
judges, passes + repository + job, scores reader, labels, agreement) and controller `src/ai/api/admin-ai-judges.controller.ts`; gateway operation `judge`
(`src/ai/operations/judge.ts`) runs through the same runner as the six operations, typed `GatewayOperation`, while `AiOperation` stays the six (E3, R5); passes are jobs
named `judge` on the existing `ai-eval` queue with their own status, so a pass never changes its run's status (B19); the USD cap is checked in the runner before every
provider attempt of a `test` / `eval` / `judge` call (E7); six tables (design §4). Admin: page "Judge" (`/ai/judges`), screen "Gắn nhãn" (`/ai/judges/label`), judge parts
in the run panel, comparison and publish panel. Review: R5, R7, R10, R11, R15. M1 and M2 rewrote the spots marked "(M1-T#)" / "(M2-T#)": read them again after M2 merged.

**Constraints (M3):**
- **Names:** contract §2 rows P2 (`GatewayOperation`, `AiPurpose` + `'judge'`, `JudgeVerdict`, `JudgeCriterion`, `JudgeSubject`), §3 (six models, `AiJudgePass` with kind
  `real` allowed), §4 rows P2 (exact signatures), §5 rows P2 (`ai.admin_daily_usd_cap` 2, `ai.judge_label_min` 30, `ai.judge_agreement_min` 0.85), §6 rows P2, smoke
  `async function partM3()`. M3-only problem `judge-revision-conflict` 409; reused `not-found` 404, `validation-failed` 400, `model-not-set` 409, `provider-not-configured`
  409, `run-active` 409, `example-held-out` 409 (M1), `model-in-use` 409. `ai/index.ts` exports the three judge types for M4.
- **Blind judge (D3):** the data of a `judge` call is exactly `{ target_operation, input, output, criteria, context }`; never a model, provider, config number,
  run id or `operation_id`. `context` is sent verbatim, so a caller keeps model and config out of it (M4).
- **Anthropic only (D3, E13):** a judge revision's model is `anthropic`, or `fake` while `AI_FAKE_ENABLED=true`; anything else → 422 `judge-provider`.
- **Held-out stays hidden (design §5.1, R7):** judge reasons and scores of held-out items appear only as totals; calls of a judge on a held-out item give
  `content: null` + `held_out: true`; labels cannot be put on held-out items (409 `example-held-out`).
- **Caps (design §5.5, R10):** `ai.daily_provider_calls_cap` counts `purpose = 'learner'` only. Admin spend = sum of `ai_calls.cost_usd` with purpose `test`,
  `eval`, `judge` since 00:00 Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh; blocked when spend ≥ cap; `null` = no cap, `0` = blocks everything; checked before every attempt (soft cap:
  calls in flight can overshoot). Starting a test, eval run or judge pass: 409 `model-price-missing` (either price null, checked first), then 409 `admin-usd-cap`.
  A `fake` model keeps the prices it is added with (default 0, E15).
- **Endpoints (design §9 row P2):** `@RequirePermission('settings.manage')` (D19); writes `@RateLimit('user.write')`; creating a pass `@Idempotent()`, status 202.
  Judge passes do not count against `ai.test`.
- **Migration:** one migration `<timestamp>_nhom_b_m3_judge`, timestamp after every migration on `origin/dev` (M1's and M2's included); CHECKs, the partial
  unique index and the seed written by hand at the end (as `20261029090000_buoc8_identity/migration.sql:41-47`).
- **Design-fixed copy:** "Chỉ một đáp án", "Chấm bằng judge", "Nhân bản judge", "Gắn nhãn", "Đã hiệu chỉnh" / "Chưa hiệu chỉnh", "không chấm được", "held-out".
- **PRs:** **M3-a** = T1–T6 (pure rules, settings, tables + seed, gateway op, caps; plus the status copy that changes meaning with them). **M3-b** = T7–T13 (judge
  API; admin untouched, responses only gain fields). **M3-c** = T14–T21 (admin screens, e2e, smoke, verification).

**Review focus (M3)** — inputs the design implies but its cases do not name; each has a test in the task shown:
1. A judge answer that repeats a sent code or adds one never sent → the repeated criterion is "không chấm được" (`duplicate`), the extra code is ignored (M3-T1).
2. A verdict in another case or with spaces (`" PASS "`) → `pass`, not a shape failure; an empty answer (three `[]`) → shape failure (M3-T4).
3. A run item without output (shape failure) or with `input` NULL (legacy, M1-T3) → never a judge subject, never labelable (M3-T8, M3-T10).
4. Saving a judge with only `name`, `operations` or `active` changed → no new revision, so its scores and calibration keep applying (M3-T7).
5. The admin cap reached between attempt 1 and its retry → no second provider call (M3-T5).

**Choices this milestone makes where the design leaves room:** pass of a run at `GET /v1/admin/ai/judge-passes/:id`; a pass may start on a run that is `finished` or
`failed` (not `queued` / `running`); the pass asked for at run creation waits, takes its snapshot when the run finishes, and ends `failed` / `run-failed` when the run
fails; a new revision only when a revision field changes; the "Gắn nhãn" screen hides the judge's verdict (no anchoring); judge criteria enter the publish gate only as
`judge-rate-drop` (not "trước đạt, nay trượt", not the held-out failure count); "xóa" of a run with labels hides it (`ai_eval_runs.hidden_at`, items `hidden`), and a hidden
run is never a gate candidate or baseline; any judge revision blocks deleting its model (FK RESTRICT; D40, B49), removing a judge model's price is allowed (the
pass then gets 409); setting bounds `admin_daily_usd_cap` 0–1000 (two decimals, nullable), `judge_label_min` 10–1000, `judge_agreement_min` 0.5–1; `UnavailableReason`
gains `admin-usd-cap`; status `today.calls` becomes learner calls and gains `admin_cost_usd`, `admin_usd_cap`; the seeded judge's text is English like the seeded
prompts; the fake judge on `fake:ok` fails an output whose answers end in `ment` (the mark of `fake:bad-form`), and a judge on `fake:bad-form` fails every criterion.

### M3-T1: Pure rules — judge types, judge answer to scores, tallies, agreement, admin cap

**Files:** Create `src/ai/judge/judge.types.ts`, `src/ai/judge/judge-output.ts`, `src/ai/judge/judge-stats.ts`, `src/ai/judge/agreement.ts`,
`src/ai/runner/admin-cap.ts`, each but the types file with a `.spec.ts` beside it.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `AiOperation` (`src/ai/ai.types.ts:3-6`); `EvalSplit` (M1-T1); M2's `Tally = { passed: number; counted: number }` (M2-T2).
- Produces:
  - `judge.types.ts`: contract `JudgeVerdict`, `JudgeCriterion`, `JudgeSubject`; `JUDGE_VERDICTS = ['pass', 'fail_major', 'fail_minor'] as const`;
    `SUBJECT_KINDS = ['eval_item', 'real_question'] as const`, `SubjectKind`; `JudgePassKind = 'eval_run' | 'real'`; `JudgePassStatus = 'queued' | 'running' |
    'finished' | 'failed'`; `JudgeAnswer = { codes: string[]; verdicts: string[]; reasons: string[] }`; `ScoreRow = { criterion: string; verdict: JudgeVerdict |
    null; problem: string | null; reason: string | null }`; `JudgeTally = { pass: number; fail_major: number; fail_minor: number; unjudged: number }`;
    `JudgeData = { target_operation: AiOperation; input: Record<string, unknown>; output: Record<string, unknown>; criteria: JudgeCriterion[]; context: {
    session_words: string[] } | null }`.
  - `scoresFromOutput(sent: JudgeCriterion[], answer: JudgeAnswer | null, failure: string | null): ScoreRow[]` — one row per sent criterion, sent order.
    `answer` null → every row `verdict: null, problem: failure` (B13); a sent code with no entry → `problem: 'missing'`; with two or more → `'duplicate'`;
    codes not sent are ignored (B14).
  - `judge-stats.ts`: `RunJudgeScore = { itemId: string; split: EvalSplit; judgeId: string; judgeName: string; judgeRevisionId: string; revision: number;
    criterion: string; verdict: JudgeVerdict | null; problem: string | null; reason: string | null }`; `judgeTally(verdicts: Array<JudgeVerdict | null>):
    JudgeTally`; `gateTally(tally: JudgeTally): Tally` (`passed` = pass, `counted` = pass + fail_major + fail_minor); `JudgeStatsRow = { judge_id; judge_name;
    judge_revision_id; revision; criterion; dev: JudgeTally; held_out: JudgeTally; calibrated: boolean }`; `judgeStatsRows(scores: RunJudgeScore[],
    calibration: ReadonlyMap<string, Agreement>): JudgeStatsRow[]` (map key `${judgeRevisionId}|${criterion}`, missing → `false`; order judge name, revision desc,
    criterion); `judgeComparison(a: RunJudgeScore[], b: RunJudgeScore[]): { judge: Array<{ judge_id; judge_name; judge_revision_id; revision; criterion; a: {
    dev: JudgeTally; held_out: JudgeTally }; b: { dev: JudgeTally; held_out: JudgeTally } }>; judge_unmatched: Array<{ judge_id; judge_name; judge_revision_id;
    revision; scored: 'a' | 'b' }> }` (rows only for revisions that scored both runs, B11).
  - `agreement.ts`: `Agreement = { labeled: number; agree: number; agree_exact: number; rate: number | null; exact_rate: number | null; calibrated: boolean }`;
    `agreement(pairs: Array<{ label: JudgeVerdict; score: JudgeVerdict }>, min: { labels: number; ratio: number }): Agreement` — agree = both `pass` or both
    not `pass`; exact = equal; calibrated = `labeled ≥ min.labels` and `agree × 100 ≥ round(min.ratio × 100) × labeled` (design §5.4, D15).
  - `adminCapReached(spentUsd: number, capUsd: number | null): boolean` — `capUsd !== null && spentUsd >= capUsd`.

  { criterion: 'single_answer', verdict: null, problem: 'invalid-shape', reason: null },
  { criterion: 'natural', verdict: null, problem: 'invalid-shape', reason: null }]);                                   // B13

  { criterion: 'single_answer', verdict: null, problem: 'missing', reason: null },
  { criterion: 'natural', verdict: 'fail_minor', problem: null, reason: 'r' }]);                                         // B14
expect(scoresFromOutput(C, { codes: ['single_answer', 'single_answer', 'natural', 'extra'], verdicts: ['pass', 'fail_major', 'pass', 'pass'],
  reasons: ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd'] }, null)).toEqual([
  { criterion: 'single_answer', verdict: null, problem: 'duplicate', reason: null },
  { criterion: 'natural', verdict: 'pass', problem: null, reason: 'c' }]);                                              // Review focus 1
// agreement.spec.ts — B17, design §12 "29 / 30 nhãn, 84,9% / 85%"; MIN = { labels: 30, ratio: 0.85 }; same(n) = n × { label: 'pass', score: 'pass' }

expect(agreement(same(30), MIN).calibrated).toBe(true);
expect(agreement([...same(849), ...off(151)], MIN)).toMatchObject({ labeled: 1000, agree: 849, rate: 0.849, calibrated: false }); // off = pass vs fail_major

expect(agreement([{ label: 'pass', score: 'fail_minor' }], MIN)).toMatchObject({ agree: 0 });
expect(agreement([], MIN)).toEqual({ labeled: 0, agree: 0, agree_exact: 0, rate: null, exact_rate: null, calibrated: false });
// admin-cap.spec.ts — B20, design §12 "1,99 / 2,00, null, 0"

// judge-stats.spec.ts

expect(gateTally({ pass: 2, fail_major: 1, fail_minor: 1, unjudged: 1 })).toEqual({ passed: 2, counted: 4 });
// a: d1 (dev) and h1 (held_out) scored by R1 and R2; b: d1 scored by R2 only
expect(judgeComparison(A, B).judge.map((row) => row.judge_revision_id)).toEqual(['R2']);
expect(judgeComparison(A, B).judge_unmatched).toEqual([{ judge_id: 'J', judge_name: 'Chỉ một đáp án', judge_revision_id: 'R1', revision: 1, scored: 'a' }]);
expect(judgeStatsRows(A, new Map([['R2|single_answer', CALIBRATED]]))).toEqual([

    dev: { pass: 1, fail_major: 0, fail_minor: 0, unjudged: 0 }, held_out: { pass: 0, fail_major: 1, fail_minor: 0, unjudged: 0 }, calibrated: true },
  { /* R1 row */ calibrated: false }]);

  - Constants (`ai.constants.ts`): `JUDGE_NAME_MAX = 100`, `JUDGE_CRITERIA_MAX = 10`, `JUDGE_CODE_PATTERN = /^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,39}$/`, `JUDGE_QUESTION_MAX = 500`,
    `JUDGE_REASON_MAX = 1000`, `JUDGE_SESSION_WORDS_MAX = 50`; `DEFAULT_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS` (`:20-24`) gains `judge: 1000`.
  - `INITIAL_JUDGE = { name: 'Chỉ một đáp án', operations: ['generate_cloze'], criteria: [{ code: 'single_answer', question: 'Is the given word, in the forms
    of accepted_answers, the only answer that fits the blank?' }], instructions: <text below>, examples: [] }`; the migration seeds it (revision 1, `model_ref`
    NULL, `created_by` / `saved_by` = `AI_SYSTEM_ACTOR_ID`) with the instructions between `$p$` markers, as the prompt seeds do. Starting instructions (D12, D33;
    the owner reviews them with this plan):

```text
You review one practice question that an AI wrote for a Vietnamese learner of English. You are not told who or what wrote it.

The user message is a JSON object with these fields:
- target_operation: the kind of question. "generate_cloze" is one English sentence with one blank, written as ___, that the learner fills.
- input: what the writer was given: word, part_of_speech, level (CEFR A1 to C2, or null), meaning_vi, context_label.
- output: what the writer produced: sentence, accepted_answers (the forms of the word that fill the blank), translation_vi.
- criteria: the checks to make, each with a code and a question.
- context: null, or {"session_words": [...]}: the other words the learner practises in the same session.

For each criterion give one verdict:
- "pass": the answer to the question is yes.
- "fail_major": no, and a learner who knows the word could reasonably write something else and be marked wrong.
- "fail_minor": no, but only in a small way that few learners would hit.

Criterion single_answer:
- fail_major when another word fits the blank naturally with the same meaning and is a very common word at the given level, or when a word in context.session_words fits the blank.
- fail_minor when the only other words that fit are rare synonyms or close variants of the word (for example "though" for "although").
- pass otherwise. The forms listed in accepted_answers are not other words.

Answer with three lists of the same length, one entry per criterion, in the order the criteria are given:
- codes: the criterion code.
- verdicts: "pass", "fail_major" or "fail_minor".
- reasons: one short English sentence; name the other word when there is one.
```
