### M3-T4: Gateway operation `judge` (E3, R5)

**Files:** Modify `src/ai/ai.types.ts`, `src/ai/operations/define-operation.ts` (`:10-29`), `src/ai/operations/operations.ts`, `src/ai/prompts/prompt-rules.ts` (`:18`),
`src/ai/runner/selection.ts` (`:9-19`, `:40`), `src/ai/runner/call-ledger.service.ts` (`:12-32`, `record` `:51-119`), `src/ai/runner/runner.service.ts` (`:23-60`,
`admin` `:179-201`, `callProvider` `:204-259`; M2-T8 changed `:129-137`, `:188-193`), `src/ai/adapters/fake/fake.adapter.ts` (`okValue` `:15-41`, `badFormValue` `:47-59`), `src/ai/index.ts`;
create `src/ai/operations/judge.ts`; specs beside each.

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `ai.types.ts`: `GATEWAY_OPERATIONS = [...AI_OPERATIONS, 'judge'] as const`, `GatewayOperation = AiOperation | 'judge'`; `AiPurpose = 'learner' | 'test' |
    'eval' | 'judge'`; `AiMeta.operation: GatewayOperation`. `OperationDefinition.name`, `OperationSpec.name`, `Selection.operation`, `CallRecord.operation` become
    `GatewayOperation`; `toSelection(operation: GatewayOperation, setting, model)`; `promptViolations(op: GatewayOperation, body)`.
  - `judgeOperation = define({ name: 'judge', … })`: input `z.strictObject({ target_operation: z.enum(AI_OPERATIONS), input: z.record(z.string(), z.unknown()),
    output: z.record(z.string(), z.unknown()), criteria: z.array(z.strictObject({ code: regex JUDGE_CODE_PATTERN, question: textIn(1, JUDGE_QUESTION_MAX) }))
    .min(1).max(JUDGE_CRITERIA_MAX), context: z.strictObject({ session_words: z.array(textIn(1, WORD_MAX)).max(JUDGE_SESSION_WORDS_MAX) }).nullable() })`; output
    `{ codes: STRING_LIST, verdicts: STRING_LIST, reasons: STRING_LIST }`, schema `judge_v1`; `normalizeAndCheck`: lengths differ → `shape`; zero entries →
    `empty`; `clean` every string; verdict lower-cased ∉ `JUDGE_VERDICTS` → `verdict`; a reason over `JUDGE_REASON_MAX` code points → `too-long`.
  - `operations.ts`: `GATEWAY_DEFINITIONS: Record<GatewayOperation, OperationDefinition> = { ...OPERATIONS, judge: judgeOperation }`; `parseOutput(op:
    GatewayOperation, text)` reads it. `OPERATIONS` keeps the six.
  - Runner: `ExecuteInput` gains `{ purpose: 'judge'; operation: 'judge'; data: unknown; actorId: string; callOperationId: string; selection: Selection; prompt:
    Prompt }` (both required: `judge` has no live config); test / eval keep `operation: AiOperation`. Input check and schema come from `GATEWAY_DEFINITIONS`.
    `CallSummary` gains `call_id: string` (the id `CallLedger.record` returns), so a judge score can name its call.
  - Fake: `okValue('judge_v1', data)` → `{ codes: criteria codes, verdicts: 'fail_major' for every criterion when some string in `data.output.accepted_answers`
    ends with `ment`, else `'pass'`, reasons: `'fake judge'` each }` (E13; design §12 "fake trả đầu ra cho thao tác judge"); `badFormValue('judge_v1', data)` → the
    same codes, every verdict `'fail_major'`, reasons `'fake: another word fits'` each, whatever the output. That is the failing judge M4 needs: real questions never
    end in `ment`, because Practice's rules drop such a cloze (`checkCloze`, `src/practice/ai/ai-checks.ts:29-35`).
  - `ai/index.ts` exports `GATEWAY_OPERATIONS`, `type GatewayOperation`, `type JudgeVerdict`, `type JudgeCriterion`, `type JudgeSubject`.

### M3-T7: Judges — list, create, save a revision, clone, revisions (B12, B34, D3, D13, E4)

**Files:** Create `src/ai/judge/judges.service.ts` (+ spec), `src/ai/api/admin-ai-judges.controller.ts` (+ spec); modify `src/ai/models/models.service.ts`
(`remove`, M2-T9) (+ spec), `src/ai/ai.module.ts`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `promptViolations('judge', …)`, `selectionViolations` (`src/ai/models/model-rules.ts:17`), `APP_CONFIG.ai.fakeEnabled`, `AiAdapterRegistry`.
- Produces:
  - `JudgeRevisionView = { id; revision; criteria: JudgeCriterion[]; instructions; examples: PromptExample[]; model: { id; provider; model_id } | null; effort;
    thinking: 'off' | null; max_output_tokens: number | null; saved_by_user: UserRef; saved_at }`; `JudgeView = { id; name; operations: AiOperation[]; active;
    created_at; latest: JudgeRevisionView }`.
  - `judgeBodySchema = z.strictObject({ name: trimmed 1..JUDGE_NAME_MAX, operations: unique AiOperation[] ≥ 1, active: boolean, criteria: 1..JUDGE_CRITERIA_MAX of
    { code: JUDGE_CODE_PATTERN, question: trimmed 1..JUDGE_QUESTION_MAX } with unique codes, instructions: string, examples: unknown[], model_ref: uuid | null,
    effort: string(1..20) | null, thinking: 'off' | null, max_output_tokens: int | null })`; `saveJudgeSchema` = it + `expected_revision: int ≥ 1`.
  - `JudgesService`: `list(): Promise<{ items: JudgeView[] }>` (oldest first); `create(actorId, body): Promise<JudgeView>` (revision 1); `save(actorId, id, body):
    Promise<JudgeView>`; `clone(actorId, id, body: { name: string }): Promise<JudgeView>` (new judge, same operations, `active: true`, revision 1 = the source's
    latest revision fields); `revisions(id): Promise<{ items: JudgeRevisionView[] }>` (newest first); `latestRevision(judgeId: string): Promise<{ id: string;
    revision: number } | null>`.
  - Check order (create, save): schema → 400 `validation-failed`; `promptViolations` → 400; unknown model → 400 `['model_ref: not found']`; provider not
    `anthropic` and not (`fake` with `fakeEnabled`) → 422 `judge-provider` (B12); provider not registered → 409 `provider-not-configured`; `selectionViolations`
    → 400. Save also: unknown judge → 404; `expected_revision` ≠ latest → 409 `judge-revision-conflict`; revision fields (criteria, instructions, examples,
    model_ref, effort, thinking, max_output_tokens) equal to the latest → only `ai_judges` is updated, no new revision (Review focus 4); else revision latest + 1
    (P2002 → 409 `judge-revision-conflict`).

**Files:** Create `src/ai/judge/judge-passes.service.ts` (+ spec), `src/ai/judge/judge-passes.repository.ts` (+ spec), `src/ai/judge/judge-scores.reader.ts`
(+ spec); modify `src/ai/api/admin-ai-eval.controller.ts` (`:66-97`), `src/ai/api/admin-ai-judges.controller.ts`, `src/ai/eval/eval-queue.service.ts` (`enqueue` `:48-57`),
`src/ai/ai.service.ts`, `src/ai/ai.module.ts`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `JudgesService.latestRevision` (M3-T7), `toSelection('judge', …)` (M3-T4), `AdminSpend.assertCanStart` (M3-T5).
- Produces:
  - `EvalQueue.enqueueJudgePass(passId: string): Promise<boolean>` — job name `judge`, data `{ passId }`, job id `judge-<passId>`, the options of `enqueue`
    (`eval-queue.service.ts:51`); false and a warn on any error. Nothing processes it until M3-T9 (a pass stays `queued`).
  - `StartableJudge = { judgeId: string; judgeRevisionId: string; revision: number; operation: AiOperation; selection: Selection }`;
    `JudgePassesService.startable(judgeId: string, op: AiOperation, now: Date): Promise<StartableJudge>` — order: unknown judge → 404; inactive → 400
    `['judge_id: judge is not active']`; `op` ∉ operations → 400 `['judge_id: judge does not score <op>']`; latest revision without model → 409 `model-not-set`;
    provider not registered → 409 `provider-not-configured`; `assertCanStart(selection.price, now)`; a `queued` / `running` pass of that revision → 409
    `judge-pass-running` (B16).
  - `JudgePassView = { id; kind: JudgePassKind; operation: AiOperation; eval_run_id: string | null; judge: { id; name }; judge_revision: { id; revision; model:
    { provider; model_id } | null }; status: JudgePassStatus; error: string | null; total: number | null; judged: number; unjudged: number; cost_usd: string | null;
    started_by_user: UserRef; created_at; started_at; finished_at }` (`unjudged` = subjects of this pass with a NULL-verdict score).
  - `createForRun(actorId, runId, body: { judge_id: string }): Promise<JudgePassView>` — body → 400; run missing or hidden → 404; run `queued` / `running` → 409
    `run-active`; `startable(…, run.operation)`; one `$transaction`: pass `{ kind: 'eval_run', operation, evalRunId, status, total }` + its items; P2002 → 409
    `judge-pass-running`; subjects = run items in position order with `status = 'succeeded'`, `input` and `output` not NULL, minus `judgedSubjects(revision,
    ids)` (B15, Review focus 3); `subject_kind 'eval_item'`, `subject_id` = item id, `context` NULL; none → pass `finished` with `total: 0`, not enqueued; else
    `queued` and `enqueueJudgePass(pass.id)`.
  - `createReal(args: { judgeId; kind: 'real'; filters: Record<string, unknown>; subjects: JudgeSubject[]; actorId }): Promise<{ passId: string }>` — `filters.operation`
    must be an `AiOperation` (400 `['filters.operation: …']`) and goes through `startable(judgeId, filters.operation, now)`, so it is one of the judge's `operations`
    (one operation per pass, M4); `limit` = `filters.limit` when an integer, else NULL; subjects minus `judgedSubjects`, none left → pass `finished` with `total: 0`, not
    enqueued; items `subject_kind 'real_question'` with `context`; the pass row's `operation` = `filters.operation`; `total` set at creation.
  - `get(passId): Promise<JudgePassView>` (404).
  - `JudgeScoresReader.judgedSubjects(judgeRevisionId: string, subjectIds: string[]): Promise<Set<string>>` — subjects whose number of non-NULL scores of that
    revision equals the revision's number of criteria; `[]` → empty set, no query.
  - `AiService.startJudgePass(args)` → `createReal`; `AiService.latestJudgeRevision(judgeId)` → `JudgesService.latestRevision`; `AiService.judgedSubjects(
    judgeRevisionId, subjectIds)` → the reader (contract §4, exact signatures).
  - Routes: `POST /v1/admin/ai/eval/runs/:id/judge-passes` (202, `@Idempotent()`), `GET /v1/admin/ai/judge-passes/:id`.

### M3-T9: The judge job, the queue, run creation with a judge, recovery (B19, B41, D3, D14)

**Files:** Create `src/ai/judge/judge-job.ts` (+ spec); modify `src/ai/judge/judge-passes.repository.ts`, `src/ai/eval/eval-queue.service.ts` (Worker `:36-45`, `enqueue` `:48-57`),
`src/ai/eval/eval-runs.service.ts` (`createRunSchema`, `create`, `cancel` `:142-149`), `src/ai/maintenance/ai-maintenance.ts` (`:25-33`, `:67-75`),
`src/ai/ai.module.ts`; specs.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `scoresFromOutput` (M3-T1), `JudgeData` (M3-T1), runner `purpose: 'judge'` (M3-T4), `startable` (M3-T8).
- Produces:
  - `JudgePassesRepository`: `start(passId, now)` (queued/running with `total` set → running, heartbeat; else null), `pendingItems(passId)` (`done = false`,
    position order), `writeScores(pass, item, rows: ScoreRow[], callId: string | null, costUsd: string | null, now)` (one transaction: `INSERT … ON CONFLICT
    (judge_revision_id, subject_kind, subject_id, criterion) DO UPDATE … WHERE ai_judge_scores.verdict IS NULL`, item `done = true`, `judged + 1`, cost added,
    heartbeat; guarded by the pass being `running`), `isRunning`, `finish`, `fail(passId, error, now)`, `release(runId, now): Promise<string[]>` (run `finished` →
    snapshot each waiting pass of the run whose `total` is NULL, return the ids of its queued passes; run `failed` → its queued passes `failed` / `run-failed`,
    return `[]`; else `[]`).
  - `JudgeJob.run(passId): Promise<void>` — revision + model → `toSelection('judge', revision, model)`; price NULL → `fail('model-price-missing')`; pool of
    `EVAL_JOB_CONCURRENCY`; each item: `runner.execute({ purpose: 'judge', operation: 'judge', data: { target_operation: pass.operation, input, output, criteria,
    context }, actorId: pass.startedBy, callOperationId: \`judge-${pass.id}-${item.id}\`, selection, prompt: { instructions, examples, revision:
    revision.revision } })`; `admin-usd-cap` → stop, `fail('admin-usd-cap')` (the run is not touched, B19); else `writeScores` with `scoresFromOutput(criteria,
    succeeded ? result : null, failure reason)`, the last call's `call_id` and summed cost.
  - `EvalQueue`: Worker dispatches on `job.name`: `'judge'` → `JudgeJob.run(job.data.passId)`; `'run'` → `EvalJob.run(runId)` then `release(runId, now)` and
    `enqueueJudgePass` (M3-T8) of each id. `EvalQueue` depends on `JudgeJob` and `JudgePassesRepository`, never on `JudgePassesService` (no DI cycle).
  - `createRunSchema` gains `judge_id: z.uuid().optional()`; `create` runs `startable(judge_id, op, now)` after its own checks and inserts the waiting pass
    (`status 'queued'`, `total` NULL) in the run's `$transaction`; P2002 → 409 `judge-pass-running`, nothing created; response `{ run_id, status: 'queued',
    judge_pass_id: string | null }`. `cancel` also sets the run's queued passes `failed` / `run-failed`.
  - Maintenance job `ai.judge-passes-recover` (hourly, with the eval recovery): queued passes older than `EVAL_QUEUED_RECOVER_MS` → `release` of their run when it
    ended, and `enqueueJudgePass` of those with `total` set; running passes with `heartbeat_at` older than `EVAL_RUNNING_STALE_MS` → `failed` / `stalled` (B41).

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing tests**

    'judge-P-I1'`; `prompt.revision: 2`. `writeScores` got the rows of `scoresFromOutput` and `call_id` `'C1'`. B19: second item `admin-usd-cap` →
    `fail('P', 'admin-usd-cap', NOW)`, no `aiEvalRun` statement. Unpriced model → `fail('P', 'model-price-missing', NOW)`, runner not called. `start` → null →

### M3-T20: `pnpm smoke:nhom-b`, part M3 (design §13, E19)

**Files:** Modify `apps/api/scripts/smoke-nhom-b.mjs` (add `async function partM3()` after M2's part; helpers of `scripts/ai-config-publish.mjs`).

**Interfaces:** Consumes M1's `setup()` (fake models, catalog), M1's frozen versions (S has at least 5 items) and M2's live `fake:ok` config of `generate_cloze`.
`partM3()` acts as a new admin made with `POST /v1/admin/users` (`staff_role: 'admin'`), so its eval runs do not share the earlier parts' `ai.test` budget (20 per
hour). Steps (J = seeded judge, S = newest frozen set of `generate_cloze`; price 1000 / 1000 USD per 1M tokens makes one fake call cost 0.15 USD, `FAKE_USAGE` 100
in + 50 out; "cap = spent + 0.10" = `status.today.admin_cost_usd` rounded up to cents + 0.10, so the first call passes and any call after one recorded call is
blocked, whatever the timing of the 3 parallel calls):
1. Seed: `GET /v1/admin/ai/judges` → one judge "Chỉ một đáp án", `operations: ['generate_cloze']`, `latest.revision: 1`, `latest.model: null`, criterion codes
   `['single_answer']`; a pass with it → 409 `model-not-set`.
2. B12: psql inserts an `openai` model row → `PUT …/judges/J` with it → 422 `judge-provider`; with `fake:ok` → revision 2; the same body with a new name → revision still
   2 (Review focus 4); `POST …/judges/J/clone` `{ name: 'Smoke clone' }` → 201, revision 1, same criteria.
3. D14, D3, B6: `POST …/eval/runs { operation: 'generate_cloze', set_id: S, config: 'live', judge_id: J }` → 202 with `judge_pass_id`; poll `GET
   …/judge-passes/:id` → `finished`, `judged` = items with output; `…/runs/R1/items` → every dev item has a `pass` score; `judge_stats[0].held_out` counts the held-out
   items; calls of a dev item's judge call → `request.data` keys exactly `context, criteria, input, output, target_operation`, and `JSON.stringify(request.data)` holds
   neither `R1` nor `fake:ok`; of a held-out item's → `content: null`, `held_out: true`.
4. B16: psql inserts a `queued` pass for the clone's revision → `POST …/runs/R1/judge-passes { judge_id: clone }` → 409 `judge-pass-running`; delete that row.
5. B15: the same pass with J on R1 again → 202, `status: 'finished'`, `total: 0`; psql count of `ai_calls` with `purpose = 'judge'` unchanged.
6. Labels: `PUT /labels` on two dev items (`pass`, `fail_major`) → 200; on a held-out item id (psql) → 409 `example-held-out`; `GET …/judges/J/agreement` → revision 2
   `single_answer` `labeled: 2, agree: 1, calibrated: false`.
7. B11: run R2 (live, S, no judge) → `compare?a=R1&b=R2` → `judge: []`, `judge_unmatched` = `[{ revision: 2, scored: 'a' }]`; pass on R2 with J → compare holds one
   judge row.
8. Gate (§6.3 step 3): a draft on `fake:bad-form`, eval on S with `judge_id: J` → `GET …/configs/generate_cloze/publish-check` warnings contain `{ code:
   'judge-rate-drop', revision: 2, criterion: 'single_answer' }` with `after.passed: 0`; delete the draft.
9. B21: `daily_provider_calls_cap` = learner calls today (`status.today.calls`) + 1, although admin calls today are far more → one `ai-fake-call` learner call
   succeeds, the next is `unavailable` / `daily-call-cap`; cap back to 3000.
10. E15, B19, B20: `POST /models { provider: 'fake', model_id: 'fake:not-recognized', price_input: 1000, price_output: 1000 }` → `price.input: '1000.0000'`; clone
    saved with it; `admin_daily_usd_cap` = spent + 0.10 → a pass of the clone on R1 ends `failed`, `error: 'admin-usd-cap'`, `1 ≤ judged ≤ 3 < total`; R1 still
    `finished`, `complete: true` (B19); then `POST …/eval/runs` → 409 `admin-usd-cap`; a new draft (kept for step 12) → `POST …/configs/generate_cloze/draft/test`
    → 409 `admin-usd-cap`.
11. B18: cap `null`; `PATCH` `fake:ok` prices 1000 / 1000; cap = spent + 0.10 → a live run on S ends `failed`, `error: 'admin-usd-cap'`, `complete: false`, `1 ≤ done
    ≤ 3 < total`; `PATCH` `fake:ok` back to 0.
12. B22, E14: `POST /models fake:refuse`, `PATCH price_input: null`; cap `null`; the clone saved with it → pass → 409 `model-price-missing`; the step-10 draft saved
    with it → `draft/test` and `POST …/eval/runs { config: 'draft' }` → 409 `model-price-missing`; delete the draft.
13. B34: `DELETE` the `fake:not-recognized` model → 409 `model-in-use`.
14. Hide: `DELETE …/runs/R1` (labelled) → 204, `GET …/runs/R1` → 404, agreement still `labeled: 2`; `DELETE …/runs/R2` (no label) → 204 and psql finds no item,
    pass or score of R2.
15. `GET /v1/admin/ai/status` → `today.admin_usd_cap: null`, `admin_cost_usd` > 0. The part leaves `admin_daily_usd_cap` `null` so later parts are not capped (the
    header of the script says so).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write** `partM3()` and call it after M2's part. **Step 2: Run** `pnpm smoke:nhom-b` on a fresh DB (the smoke run) → exit 0; a step that fails on a
  code defect is fixed the repo's way (failing unit test in the owning task's spec, then the fix, two commits). **Step 3: Commit** `test(api): add the judge part of
  the nhóm b smoke`.

---

## Milestone M4 — Real questions (design K4)

**Goal:** the admin judges up to 200 real AI questions of the last 7 days with a judge, sees each question's verdict and reason, labels them, and turns a failed real
question or a confirmed AI report into a `dev` eval example with its expected answer filled in. Practice owns the questions, so Practice selects them and builds the
snapshots (`src/practice/quality/**`, new); `ai` runs the pass (M3's `startJudgePass`, worker `ai-eval`, USD cap, scores) and stores everything (E8, R8). Practice reaches
`ai` only through `AiService` (contract §4 plus the four reads of M4-T1), Identity through `IdentityService.staffUserIds()`, Content through `ContentService`. The "add to
examples" endpoint sits in Practice and ends in M1's `AiService.createEvalExample`. Admin: page "Câu AI thật" (`/ai/real-questions`), one dialog shared with the report
detail, and real questions in "Gắn nhãn". **No migration.** Review: R6, R8, R15, R19. Read `src/ai/**` again after M3 merged.

**Constraints (M4):**
- **Module boundaries (D3, R8):** Practice never queries `ai_*` tables nor `users`; `ai` never queries `practice_*`; the reader in `src/ai/judge/` reads only M3's tables.
- **Private words never reach a judge or the example set (design §7.1, R6, B39):** a question is skipped when the asked word, any option, or **anything sent to the AI**
  (every MCQ `candidates` sense, every wrong meaning, a private `contentKey`) is a private word. `context.session_words` holds catalog lemmas only. No response of a
  new endpoint carries a learner id; logs never carry input, output or sentences.
- **Source window (design §7.1, B38):** `practice_questions` with `source = 'ai'` and `created_at ≥ now − content_retention_days × 86 400 000 ms` (the arithmetic of
  `call-ledger.service.ts:90-99`); a question needs both its `practice_ai_requests.ai_input` (kept `AI_REQUEST_RETENTION_DAYS` = 7, `practice-cleanup.service.ts:58-60`) and
  the content of `ai_meta.call_operation_id` (`AiService.callContents`), else it counts as `no_content`. `content_retention_days = 0` → no source, reason `retention-off`.
- **One operation per pass:** `operation` ∈ `generate_cloze`, `generate_meaning_question`, `generate_sentence_mcq`, required, one of the judge's `operations`; sent to
  M3 as `filters.operation` (M3-T8). Pair filter = `ai_meta->>'prompt_revision'` (the config number since M2, design §6.5) and `ai_meta->>'model'`, both or neither.
  "Chỉ tài khoản test" = `user_id ∈ IdentityService.staffUserIds()`, read on every estimate and every start (current role, D35, B47).
- **Selection:** newest first (`created_at DESC, id DESC`), not yet judged by the judge's latest revision (`judgedSubjects`; a subject whose only scores are "không chấm
  được" is judged again, M3-T8), at most `ai.realJudgeLimit`. The start request selects again; it never reuses the estimate's list.
- **Setting:** `ai.realJudgeLimit` / `ai.real_judge_limit`, default 200 (contract §5), bounds 1–500, integer.
- **Problems:** reused `not-found` 404, `validation-failed` 400; M3's `judge-pass-running`, `admin-usd-cap`, `model-price-missing` and M1's `expected-invalid` pass
  through unchanged. M4-only: `nothing-to-judge` 409 (extensions `reason: 'retention-off' | 'no-staff' | 'empty'`, `skipped`), `source-not-eligible` 409 (extension
  `reason: 'report-not-confirmed' | 'not-ai' | 'private-word' | 'sense-gone' | 'no-input'`).
- **Endpoints (design §9 row P4)**, all `@RequirePermission('settings.manage')` (D19, B43); writes `@RateLimit('user.write')` and `@Idempotent()`:
  `GET /v1/admin/practice/real-judge/estimate`, `POST /v1/admin/practice/real-judge` (201), `GET …/real-judge/passes`, `GET …/real-judge/passes/:id/items`,
  `GET /v1/admin/practice/eval-examples/from-source` (preview, added by this plan), `POST /v1/admin/practice/eval-examples/from-source` (201).
- **Scale (`ASSUMPTION`, as E9):** the window query reads every AI question of the window (≤ 30 days) without a new index; fine at today's volume.
- **PRs:** **M4-A** = T1–T7 (API, additive: new endpoints and one setting). **M4-B** (after M4-A merged, branch rebased) = T8–T12 (admin, smoke, verification).

**Review focus (M4)** — inputs the design implies but no case names; each has a test in the task shown:
1. An MCQ question whose private word was among the `candidates` sent to the AI but not among the three distractors chosen → still skipped (M4-T4).
2. A question made by the second call (`call_operation_id = <id>#2`) → the judged output is that call's content, not the rejected first answer (M4-T5).
3. A role or `content_retention_days` changed between estimate and start → the start uses the new values (M4-T5).
4. A confirmed report whose question row was already cleaned up → privacy is read from the snapshot's masked options and the sense's scope (M4-T7).
5. A pass whose questions were deleted later (account deletion, `history_months`) → items still listed from the snapshot, no "Đưa vào bộ ví dụ" (M4-T6).

**Names M5 uses from M4:** Practice `metaOf` (M4-T4) parses `ai_meta` for the dashboard (M5-T8); a real pass's `filters` = `{ operation, config, test_accounts_only,
limit, since, skipped }` (M4-T5).

### M4-T1: `ai` reads for real-question passes

**Files:** Create `src/ai/judge/real-passes.reader.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`); modify `src/ai/ai.service.ts` (+ `ai.service.spec.ts`), `src/ai/ai.module.ts` (provider),
`src/ai/index.ts`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: M3's tables (contract §3); `latestJudgeRevision` (contract §4); `encodeBoundToken` / `decodeBoundToken` (`src/common/pagination/page-token.util.ts`).
- Produces (types in `real-passes.reader.ts`, exported from `ai/index.ts` with `JudgeSubject`, `JudgeVerdict`, `JudgeCriterion` if M3 did not):
  - `RealJudgeTarget = { judgeId: string; name: string; operations: AiOperation[]; active: boolean; revisionId: string; revision: number }`.
  - `RealPassView = { id; judge: { id: string; name: string }; revision: number; revision_id: string; criteria: JudgeCriterion[]; judge_model: { provider: string;
    model_id: string } | null; status: 'queued' | 'running' | 'finished' | 'failed'; error: string | null; total: number; judged: number; cost_usd: string | null;
    filters: Record<string, unknown>; started_by: string; created_at: string; finished_at: string | null }` (`total` is never NULL for a `real` pass: it takes its
    snapshot at creation, M3-T8).
  - `RealPassItem = { subject_id: string; input: Record<string, unknown>; output: Record<string, unknown>; context: { session_words: string[] } | null; scores:
    Array<{ criterion: string; verdict: JudgeVerdict | null; problem: string | null; reason: string | null }>; labels: Array<{ criterion: string; verdict: JudgeVerdict }> }`.
  - `AiService.realJudgeTarget(judgeId: string): Promise<RealJudgeTarget | null>` — null for an unknown judge or one with no revision.
  - `AiService.judgeCostPerSubject(revisionId: string): Promise<number | null>` — `Σ cost_usd / Σ judged` over that revision's passes (any kind) with `judged > 0`
    and `cost_usd` not null; none → null (design §7.1 "chưa ước tính được").
  - `AiService.realJudgePasses(pageSize: number, pageToken?: string): Promise<{ items: RealPassView[]; next_page_token: string | null }>` — `kind = 'real'` only,
    newest first; a bad token → 400 `validation-failed` (as `eval-runs.service.ts:103-127`).
  - `AiService.realJudgePass(passId: string): Promise<{ pass: RealPassView; items: RealPassItem[] } | null>` — null when unknown or not `real`; `scores` = rows of
    `(pass's revision, 'real_question', subject)`; `labels` = `ai_labels` of `('real_question', subject)`.

    `realJudgeBodySchema` (strict; `config` nullable) and `realJudgeQuerySchema` (`judge_id`, `operation`, `config_number` int ≥ 0 and `model` 1–200 chars both or
    neither, `test_accounts_only` `'true' | 'false'` default false) → `RealJudgeFilters`.
  - `Skipped = { private_word: number; no_content: number; already_judged: number }`; `RealJudgeEstimate = { judge_revision: number; selected: number; remaining: number;
    limit: number; skipped: Skipped; estimate_usd: number | null; admin_usd_cap: number | null; reason: 'retention-off' | 'no-staff' | null; retention_days: number;
    pairs: Array<{ config_number: number | null; model: string; questions: number }> }`.
  - `RealQuestionsRepository.window(args: { since: Date; operation: PracticeAiOperation; config: { number: number; model: string } | null; userIds: string[] | null }):
    Promise<RealQuestionRow[]>` — one `$queryRaw`: `practice_questions q LEFT JOIN practice_ai_requests r ON r.id::text = q.ai_request_id LEFT JOIN practice_sessions s ON
    s.id = r.session_id`, `q.source = 'ai'`, `q.created_at >= since`, `q.ai_meta->>'operation' = op`, optional `(q.ai_meta->>'prompt_revision')::int`, `q.ai_meta->>'model'`,
    `q.user_id = ANY(userIds)`, `ORDER BY q.created_at DESC, q.id DESC`; `aiInput` = `r.ai_input`, `sessionSenseIds` = `s.sense_ids` or `[]`.
    `pairs(args: { since; operation; userIds }): Promise<Array<{ config_number; model; questions }>>` — grouped, most questions first.
  - `RealJudgeService.estimate(actorId: string, f: RealJudgeFilters): Promise<RealJudgeEstimate>` and `start(actorId: string, f: RealJudgeFilters): Promise<{ pass_id:
    string; selected: number; skipped: Skipped }>`, both over one private `select(actorId, f)`: judge check (unknown → 404; `f.operation ∉ operations` → 400
    `['operation: not judged by this judge']`; inactive → 400 `['judge_id: judge is not active']`); retention 0 → `retention-off`; test-only with no staff → `no-staff`;
    rows → `touchesPrivateWord` → `private_word`; `aiInput` null → `no_content`; `judgedSubjects(revisionId, rest)` → `already_judged`; then the eligible rows in order, in
    chunks of `limit`, through `callContents(call_operation_ids)` + `parseOutput` (missing or unreadable → `no_content`) until `limit` subjects; `remaining` = eligible rows
    not examined. One `practiceCandidates(actorId, <union of session sense ids>, 'vi')` gives `sessionWords`. `start` with no subject → 409 `nothing-to-judge`
    (`reason` `retention-off` / `no-staff` / `empty`), no AI call; else `startJudgePass({ judgeId, kind: 'real', filters: { operation, config, test_accounts_only, limit,
    since: <ISO>, skipped }, subjects, actorId })` → 201 `{ pass_id, selected, skipped }`.
  - Routes on `@Controller('admin/practice/real-judge')` (`settings.manage`): `GET estimate`, `POST ''` (201, `@Idempotent()`, `IdempotencyInterceptor`, `user.write`).

### M4-T7: "Đưa vào bộ ví dụ" from a confirmed report or a real question (D27, E17, B39, B42, B43)

**Files:** Create `src/practice/quality/example-source.service.ts` (+ spec), `src/practice/quality/admin-example-source.controller.ts` (+ spec); modify
`real-questions.repository.ts` (+ spec), `real-judge.types.ts`, `src/practice/practice.module.ts`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `AiService.createEvalExample` (contract §4, always `split: 'dev'`); `PracticeEvalRules.formsOf` (M1-T6, same module); `touchesPrivateWord`, `maskedOptionIn`,
  `metaOf` (M4-T4); `clozeInput`, `meaningInput`, `mcqInput` (`src/practice/ai/ai-inputs.ts:19-26`); `pickWrongMeanings`, `pickMcqForm` (`src/practice/ai/ai-checks.ts:101,
  122`); `eligibleCandidates` (`src/practice/questions/distractors.ts:48`); `ContentService.practiceSenses`, `catalogPracticeSensesByLemma` (M1), `practiceRandomMeanings`,
  `practiceLemmaMeanings` (`content.service.ts:220-245`).
- Produces:
  - `SourceRef = { report_id: string } | { question_id: string }`; `SourcePreview = { operation: PracticeAiOperation; source: 'report' | 'real_judge'; source_ref: string;
    word: string; part_of_speech: string; input: Record<string, unknown> | null; rebuilt: boolean; expected: { forms: string[] } | null }`.
  - `RealQuestionsRepository.source(ref: SourceRef): Promise<SourceRow | null>`, `SourceRow = { senseId: string; ownerId: string; aiMeta: unknown; questionSource: string; reportStatus:
    string | null; snapshot: unknown | null; answerKey: AnswerKey | null; aiInput: StoredAiInput | null }` — a report finds its question by `ai_request_id =
    ai_operation_id` and its request by id; either may be gone; `ownerId` = the report's or the question's `user_id`.
  - `ExampleSourceService.preview(actorId: string, ref: SourceRef): Promise<SourcePreview>` — order: unknown → 404; report not `confirmed` → 409 `source-not-eligible`
    `report-not-confirmed`; not `ai` → `not-ai`; question row present → `touchesPrivateWord({ answerKey, aiInput })`, absent → `maskedOptionIn(snapshot)` → `private-word`;
    `aiInput` present → `input` = `aiInput.data`, `rebuilt: false`; else rebuild: the sense through `practiceSenses(row.ownerId, [senseId], 'vi')` (read as the learner who
    was asked: a private sense is visible only to its owner, `src/content/learner/visible-senses.ts:9-15`), none → `sense-gone`, `scope 'private'` → `private-word`, `input: null`, `rebuilt: true`. `word`, `part_of_speech` from the input (or the sense); `expected` = `{ forms }` of
    `formsOf(word, part_of_speech)` for cloze and meaning (null forms → `expected: null`), `null` for MCQ.
  - `ExampleSourceService.rebuildInput(op: PracticeAiOperation, sense: PracticeSense, actorId: string, random: () => number = Math.random): Promise<Record<string, unknown>>`
    — cloze `clozeInput(sense)`; meaning: `pickWrongMeanings(sense, <catalogPracticeSensesByLemma(lemma, pos, 'vi') without the sense>, [], <practiceRandomMeanings(actorId,
    [lemma], 3, 'vi')>, <practiceLemmaMeanings>)`; MCQ: `pickMcqForm(sense, eligibleCandidates(sense, <practiceRandomMeanings(actorId, [lemma], 12, 'vi')>, Infinity,
    meanings), random)`; no pick or no `gloss_vi` → 409 `source-not-eligible` `no-input`. No group mates: catalog only (E17).
  - `ExampleSourceService.create(actorId: string, body: { report_id?: string; question_id?: string; expected: Record<string, unknown> | null; note: string | null }):
    Promise<{ id: string; operation: PracticeAiOperation; rebuilt: boolean }>` — `preview` again, `rebuildInput` when `rebuilt`, then `createEvalExample({ operation, input,
    expected: body.expected, note: body.note, source: 'report' | 'real_judge', sourceRef: <report or question id>, actorId })`; its 422 `expected-invalid` passes through.
  - Routes on `@Controller('admin/practice/eval-examples')` (`settings.manage`): `GET from-source?report_id|question_id` (exactly one, uuid), `POST from-source` (201,
    `@Idempotent()`, `user.write`; body strict, exactly one id, `note` trimmed ≤ 500 or null).

    was called with that id, not the admin's; sense not visible to the learner → `sense-gone`.

### M4-T11: `partM4()` in `apps/api/scripts/smoke-nhom-b.mjs` (design §13)

**Files:** Modify `apps/api/scripts/smoke-nhom-b.mjs` (add `async function partM4()`, called after M3's part).

**Interfaces:** Consumes the smoke's helpers (`step`, `ok`, `check`, `want`, `wantProblem`, `psql`), M2's `publishModel` (`scripts/ai-config-publish.mjs`), M1's catalog verbs
W1–W4, the judge M3 seeds ("Chỉ một đáp án", `generate_cloze`), the failing fake judge `fake:bad-form` (M3-T4), practice helpers as `scripts/smoke-buoc-7.mjs:205, 304-320, 433-438, 451`.

Steps of `partM4()` (it acts as a new admin, Global Constraints "`ai.test` budget"; E = editor, L = learner, both made with `POST /v1/admin/users` (`staff_role`
`'editor'` / `null`, no registration spent), `native_language` `vi`, Pro with AI units and 200 sessions a day, E's token kept for `partM5()`; `fake:ok` published for the four
Practice operations; E has a private word P):
1. Setup: the judge saved with `fake:bad-form` (new revision; every verdict `fail_major`, M3-T4); E practises AI cloze on W1–W3 (with `prepare`), AI cloze on P, an MCQ sentence on W1 whose three mates
   are W2, W3, P (as smoke-buoc-7 Review Focus 5); L practises AI cloze on W1, W2. psql: 6 `generate_cloze` questions, 1 MCQ. Note `count(*)` of `ai_calls` with `purpose =
   'judge'` as J0.
2. B38: settings `ai.content_retention_days = 0` → estimate `reason: 'retention-off'`, `selected: 0`; POST → 409 `nothing-to-judge`; restore 7.
3. Estimate (`generate_cloze`, all accounts) → `selected: 5`, `skipped.private_word: 1`, `estimate_usd: null`, `pairs` `[{ config_number: <live cloze number>, model:
   'fake:ok', questions: 6 }]`; test-only → `selected: 3`.
4. B40 + D35: `PUT /v1/admin/users/E/staff-role` `{ staff_role: null }` → the staff left (the admins) practised nothing: test-only estimate `selected: 0`, `reason: null`;
   POST → 409 `nothing-to-judge`, `reason: 'empty'`; judge calls still J0; role back to `editor` → test-only `selected: 3` again (current role, B47). (`no-staff` itself is
   unit-tested in M4-T5: the smoke always has an admin.)
5. B16: psql inserts a `running` real pass for the judge's revision → POST → 409 `judge-pass-running`; delete that row.
6. Test-only POST → 201 `selected: 3`; poll `GET …/real-judge/passes` until `finished` (≤ 60 s); `GET …/passes/:id/items` → 3 items of E, each `failed: true`,
   `can_add_to_examples: true`, `context.session_words` = the other two of W1–W3; no item and no `ai_judge_pass_items` row (psql) contains P's lemma. Estimate test-only →
   `selected: 0`, `skipped.already_judged: 3`.
7. All accounts POST → 201 `selected: 2` (L's), finished.
8. From a real-judge row: preview `question_id` = item 1 (word W) → `source: 'real_judge'`, `rebuilt: false`, `expected.forms` sorted = `[W, Wed, Ws]`; POST → 201; `GET
   /v1/admin/ai/eval/examples?operation=generate_cloze` has it with `split: 'dev'`, `source: 'real_judge'`, `source_ref` = item 1, the preview's input. B39: preview of the MCQ
   question → 409 `source-not-eligible`, `reason: 'private-word'`.
9. Report: L reports its cloze item (`ai_more_than_one_answer`); preview → 409 `report-not-confirmed`; E confirms it; B43: E's GET and POST `…/from-source` → 403; admin
   preview → `source: 'report'`, `rebuilt: false`; POST → 201 with `source: 'report'`, `source_ref` = the report id.
10. B42: psql deletes that report's `practice_ai_requests` row (what the hourly cleanup does after 7 days) → preview `rebuilt: true`, `input: null`; POST → 201 `rebuilt: true`; the
    example's input = `{ word, part_of_speech, level, meaning_vi, context_label }` of the reported word's catalog sense.
11. B41: psql inserts a real pass `running` with `heartbeat_at` 16 minutes ago; `pnpm maintenance:run` → the pass list shows it `failed`, `error: 'stalled'`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write** `partM4()`. **Step 2: Run** it as the smoke run (Global Constraints) — every step `ok`. A step failing on a code defect: red unit test in
  the owning task's spec, then the fix (two commits), then the smoke again.
- [ ] **Step 3: Commit** `test(api): add the real questions part of the nhom b smoke`.

| §7.1 filters, `staffUserIds`, current role (D35, B40, B47) | M4-T3, M4-T5, M4-T9, M4-T11 |
| §7.1 limit, newest first, not yet judged, estimate, one pass per revision (B16), stalled (B41) | M4-T2, M4-T5, M4-T11 |
| §7.1 results page, labels; §5.4 "Gắn nhãn" for real questions | M4-T6, M4-T9, M4-T10 |
| §7.2 button, endpoint, input rebuilt (E17, B42), expected filled, `dev`, admin only (B43) | M4-T7, M4-T8, M4-T11 |
| §9 row P4, §4 setting `real_judge_limit` | M4-T5 … M4-T7, M4-T9 |
| §12 tests, §13 smoke | every task; M4-T11, M4-T12 |


---

## Milestone M5 — Dashboard (design K5)

**Goal:** the `/dashboard` page (`ai.view`) answers four more questions (design §8, D29): on which days learners hit the AI cap of that day; how the latest complete
eval of each live config scored; what the judge says per operation and (config number, model) pair, from eval and from real questions, and whether that judge is
calibrated; and the latest finished real-question pass per operation. The "today" block shows learner calls against the learner call cap and admin USD against the admin
USD cap (design §5.5; the API fields come from M3-T5). The `ai-quality` report also counts `confirmed` reports (F11). Each block is read by the module that owns its data
and reaches the admin through the three existing endpoints, fields added only (design §9 row P5): Settings gains a read of one setting's history, which Entitlements uses
for each day's cap; `ai` gains `DashboardQualityService` (`src/ai/reports/`) and `AiService.realJudgeScores` (precedent `AiService.secondTries`, `src/ai/ai.service.ts:65`);
Practice maps those scores to its questions' `ai_meta` with M4's `metaOf` (F7). The admin draws one more hand-made chart in `features/dashboard/charts.tsx` (D28) and puts
the three new cards in `features/dashboard/QualityCards.tsx`; `features/dashboard/retention*` is left for M6. No chart library (D28), no migration. Review: R10.5, R12,
R18. M1–M4 change several of these files first: read them again when a task starts.

**Constraints (M5):**
- **Module boundaries (design §9 "Hàm mới giữa các module"):** only Settings reads `app_setting_changes`; only `ai` reads `ai_*`; only Practice reads `practice_*`.
  Practice gets judge data only from `AiService.realJudgeScores`; Entitlements gets cap history only from `SettingsService.valuesAt`.
- **Additive API (SR7, design §9 row P5):** new fields only; every existing field keeps its name and type. One existing value changes meaning on purpose:
  `days_at_cap` now counts each day against that day's own cap (design §8.1 "hiện nay so với trần hiện tại"; the comment `entitlements.service.ts:198-201` names the old
  rule a limitation).
- **Permissions:** the three endpoints keep `@RequirePermission('ai.view')` (admin and editor); no new endpoint.
- **Totals only (design §5.1 R7, `dashboard.note`):** no example input or output, no example or item id, no question text and no learner id leaves these endpoints;
  held-out results are counts.
- **Days:** Vietnam days (`Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh`) for cap days, "today" and the range, as the endpoints already do.
- **Pair (R12):** judge numbers are grouped by (config number, model), never by config number alone.
- **Design-fixed copy:** "Đã hiệu chỉnh" / "Chưa hiệu chỉnh", "held-out".
- **PRs:** **M5-a** = T1–T8 (API). **M5-b** (after M5-a merged and rebased) = T9–T12 (admin, smoke, verification). Between the two merges the usage card's hint still says
  "giới hạn hệ thống hiện hành" while the number already uses each day's cap; M5-T9 fixes the text. F11 is a defect fix in two commits (M5-T7).

**Review focus (M5)** — inputs the design implies but its cases do not name; each has a test in the task shown:
1. A run of the live config made while it was still a draft (older `config_version`, older text) → never shown as the live config's latest eval and never counted in
   its judge group (M5-T4, M5-T5).
2. A run "deleted" while it had labels (`hidden_at` set, items `hidden`, M3-T11) → gone from the latest eval and from the judge groups (M5-T4, M5-T5).
3. A day whose cap was 0 with 0 uses → counts as at cap (AI was off all day; same `total ≥ cap` as today); a cap change at exactly 00:00 belongs to the new day (M5-T1, M5-T2).
4. A real-question score whose question no longer exists (account deleted) → left out, the other rows unchanged, no error (M5-T8).
5. Admin USD exactly at the cap (2,00 of 2) → the card says the cap is reached, as the gateway blocks at `≥` (B20) (M5-T9).

**Choices this milestone makes where the design leaves room:** the value of a setting on a day is the `new_value` of its last change before the next Vietnam midnight,
before its first change that change's `old_value`, with no change the current value; `days_at_cap` keeps its name and type; "bản đang chạy" in §8.2 means `config_id` =
live **and** `config_version` = that config's version; eval judge groups use each operation's newest frozen set, real groups the dashboard range; one judge chart per
(operation, judge, criterion) with a marker where the revision changes; "lượt chấm gần nhất" = the newest **finished** real pass in the range.

### M5-T1: The value a setting had at given instants (Settings)

**Files:** Create `src/settings/setting-history.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`), `src/settings/settings.service.spec.ts`; modify `src/settings/settings.definitions.ts` (beside
`changedFields` `:278-284`), `src/settings/settings.service.ts` (beside `history` `:86-110`), `src/settings/index.ts`.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `FIELDS` (`settings.definitions.ts:197-241`), `SettingsService.current()` (`:43-46`), model `AppSettingChange` (`prisma/models/settings.prisma:13-23`).
- Produces:
  - `type SettingKeyChange = { oldValue: unknown; newValue: unknown; changedAt: Date }`; `settingValueAt(changes: SettingKeyChange[], current: unknown, instant: Date):
    unknown` — `changes` oldest first; the `newValue` of the last change with `changedAt < instant`; when none, the `oldValue` of the first change (the value in force
    before any save, design B44 "trước lần lưu đầu tiên"); with no change at all, `current`.
  - `apiValueOf(values: AppSettings, key: string): boolean | number | string | null | undefined` — `key` as history rows name it (`<section>.<api_key>`);
    `undefined` for an unknown key.
  - `SettingsService.valuesAt(key: string, instants: Date[]): Promise<unknown[]>` — unknown key → `throw new Error('Unknown setting key <key>')` before any read; one
    `appSettingChange.findMany({ where: { key }, orderBy: [{ changedAt: 'asc' }, { id: 'asc' }], select: { oldValue: true, newValue: true, changedAt: true } })` and one
    `current()`; one value per instant, in order. `index.ts` exports `type SettingKeyChange`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

```ts
// setting-history.spec.ts — C(at, old, new); END(d) = the Vietnam midnight after day d (UTC+7)
const CHANGES = [C('2026-10-17T05:00:00Z', 1000, 3), C('2026-10-19T03:00:00Z', 3, 5), C('2026-10-19T08:00:00Z', 5, 7)];
expect(settingValueAt([], 9, END('2026-10-17'))).toBe(9);                            // never changed → current
expect(settingValueAt(CHANGES, 7, END('2026-10-16'))).toBe(1000);                    // before the first change → its old value (B44)
expect(settingValueAt(CHANGES, 7, new Date('2026-10-17T05:00:00Z'))).toBe(1000);     // strictly before
expect(settingValueAt(CHANGES, 7, END('2026-10-17'))).toBe(3);
expect(settingValueAt(CHANGES, 7, END('2026-10-18'))).toBe(3);
expect(settingValueAt(CHANGES, 7, END('2026-10-19'))).toBe(7);                       // two changes that day → the last value (B44)
expect(settingValueAt([C('2026-10-17T17:00:00Z', 1000, 3)], 3, END('2026-10-17'))).toBe(1000); // at 00:00 → the next day (Review focus 3)
// settings.service.spec.ts — fake prisma: rows of CHANGES, appSetting { version: 3, value: { entitlements: { aiDailyCap: 7 } } }
expect(await service.valuesAt('entitlements.ai_daily_cap', [END('2026-10-16'), END('2026-10-20')])).toEqual([1000, 7]);
expect(prisma.appSettingChange.findMany).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ where: { key: 'entitlements.ai_daily_cap' },
  orderBy: [{ changedAt: 'asc' }, { id: 'asc' }], select: { oldValue: true, newValue: true, changedAt: true } });
await expect(service.valuesAt('entitlements.nope', [END('2026-10-16')])).rejects.toThrow('Unknown setting key entitlements.nope');
expect(prisma.appSettingChange.findMany).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);                   // the unknown key read nothing
expect(apiValueOf(SETTINGS_DEFAULTS, 'entitlements.ai_daily_cap')).toBe(1000);
```

### M5-T11: `pnpm smoke:nhom-b`, part M5 (design §13 "đọc dashboard")

**Files:** Modify `apps/api/scripts/smoke-nhom-b.mjs` (add `async function partM5()` after M4's part and call it; contract §7).

**Interfaces:** Consumes the script's helpers and accounts (`setup()`: `editor`, learner; E and its token from `partM4()`; `psql`; the run-wait helper of M1's part); the
part acts as a new admin (Global Constraints, "`ai.test` budget"); M2's live config and
`GET /v1/admin/ai/configs/:op`; M3's seeded judge "Chỉ một đáp án" on model `fake` and its endpoints (design §9 row P2); the finished real pass M4's part leaves (its judge on `fake:bad-form`). Each step
checks status, `type` and body against an independent read (psql or another endpoint), never against values the step itself wrote into the read under test.

Steps of `partM5()`:
1. Save `ai.admin_daily_usd_cap` and `entitlements.ai_daily_cap` (*C0*) from `GET /v1/admin/settings`; set `admin_daily_usd_cap` to `null` (M3's part may leave the cap reached).
2. Permissions: the `editor` gets 200 from the three endpoints and sees the new keys (`latest_evals`, `judge_scores`, `judge_revisions`,
   `real_judge_scores`, `real_judge_latest`, `reports[].confirmed`, `days[].cap`); the learner gets 403 from each.
3. Today (§5.5): `today.calls` = psql count of `purpose = 'learner'` calls since Vietnam midnight; `today.admin_cost_usd` = psql sum of `cost_usd` of `test`, `eval`,
   `judge` since then (`numeric(20,8)::text`); `admin_usd_cap` `null`.
4. Latest eval (§8.2): *S* = newest set of `generate_cloze` (`GET …/eval/sets?operation=generate_cloze`); run the live config on *S* (`POST …/eval/runs { operation, set_id,
   config: 'live' }`), wait `finished` → `latest_evals` entry: `run.id` = it, `set.version` = *S*'s, `live.number` = `configs/generate_cloze` `live.number`, `run.stats`
   deep-equals `GET …/runs/:id/items` `stats`, `Object.keys(run)` exactly `id, created_at, model, stats, judge`; step 3's `today.calls` unchanged (B21). Then a draft
   (`POST …/configs/generate_cloze/draft`, or the one in `draft-exists`), run it on *S*, wait → `latest_evals` still names the live run; delete the draft only if this step made it.
5. Judge (§8.3, D17): a judge pass on step 4's run with the seeded judge → finished; `run.judge` counts = psql counts of that run's scores by split and verdict. Save a new
   revision of the judge (`PUT …/judges/:id` with `max_output_tokens` changed, since equal fields make no revision, M3-T7) → `latest.revision` + 1; judge the same run
   again → `judge_scores` has a group of (live number, run model) for each of the two
   revisions, each equal to psql counts over *S* for that revision; `judge_revisions` lists both; each `criteria[].calibrated` equals the D15 rule recomputed from psql
   (`ai_labels` × `ai_judge_scores`, `ai.judge_label_min`, `ai.judge_agreement_min`).
6. Real questions (§8.4): psql finds at least one finished real pass with a score today (else stop: "M4's part left no finished real pass"); `real_judge_latest` for
   `generate_cloze` names the finished pass with the newest score and its counts = psql; the `real_judge_scores` group of (`ai_meta` number, model) = psql join of
   `ai_judge_scores` and `practice_questions`.
7. F11: an AI item E (of `partM4()`) was asked and has not reported (psql) → E `POST /v1/practice/items/:id/report { reason: 'ai_more_than_one_answer' }` → admin
   `POST /v1/admin/practice/reports/:id/resolve { status: 'confirmed' }` → the `ai` row of `reports`: `confirmed` +1, `total` +1, `open` unchanged.
8. Days at cap (§8.1, B44; needs *C0* > 3 and today's total < *C0*, true for the default 1000): psql inserts two `app_setting_changes` rows for `entitlements.ai_daily_cap` — *C0* → 3 at 12:00 Vietnam of today − 3, 3 → *C0* at 12:00 of
   today − 1 — and confirmed `ai_lookup` uses for the learner (`system_date`, `confirmed_at`, `operation_id` `smoke-m5-<run>-<n>`): 3 on today − 3, 2 on today − 2, 3 on
   today − 1. `PUT /v1/admin/settings` cap 4 → today's day `cap: 4`; PUT cap *C0*. `GET …/ai-usage/days?from=<today − 4>&to=<today>` → `cap` `[C0, 3, 3, C0, C0]`, `at_cap`
   `[false, true, false, false, false]`, `days_at_cap: 1` (each day against *C0* would give 0).
9. Restore `ai.admin_daily_usd_cap`.

- [ ] **Step 1:** Write `partM5()`. **Step 2:** Run it as the smoke run (Global Constraints) — every step `ok`. A step that fails on a code defect is fixed the repo's
  way: a failing unit test in the owning task's spec, then the fix (two commits). **Step 3: Commit** `test(api): add the dashboard part of the nhom b smoke`.

## Milestone M6 — D7/D30 (design K6)

**Goal:** the dashboard says how many learners are "đang học" and "đã ngừng" today and, per weekly registration cohort, how many were still learning 7 and 30
days after they signed up (design §10, D6, D36, D39). Learning owns a new table `learning_activity_days` (one row per learner per local day with a "lần học") and
writes it beside every `learning_log` write; Practice writes it through `LearningService.recordActivity` when it records a final answer (Practice already imports
`LearningModule`, `src/practice/practice.module.ts:7, 40`, and holds `LearningService` in `src/practice/answers/answers.service.ts:54`). Learning reads registrations
through `IdentityService.registrations` (contract §4) and staff through M4's `staffUserIds`, computes everything at read time (E9) and serves
`GET /v1/admin/learning/retention` (`ai.view`, E18). The admin draws one more card on `/dashboard` from `features/dashboard/retention*` (D28: no chart library) and
gets one more field on Cài đặt. Review: R3, R22. M1–M5 do not touch `src/learning/**`; read `src/practice/answers/answers.service.ts`, `src/identity/profile/identity.service.ts`
and `features/dashboard/DashboardPage.tsx` again after M5 merged.

**Constraints (M6):**
- **Module boundaries (D3):** only Learning reads or writes `learning_activity_days`; Learning reads users only through `IdentityService.registrations` and
  `IdentityService.staffUserIds`; Practice writes activity only through `LearningService.recordActivity`.
- **Names:** contract §4 `IdentityService.registrations(from: Date, to: Date)` exactly; contract §5 `learning.activeWindowDays` / `learning.active_window_days`,
  default 7.
- **What counts as a "lần học" (D39, design §10):** every `learning_log` write (flashcard review, "Cần ôn tập" / "Đã biết" from practice, words added or removed,
  "Học lại") and every final practice answer. The row is written with `INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` (Prisma `createMany({ skipDuplicates: true })`), in the
  transaction of the write it marks and as that transaction's **last write**, with the same local date that write uses. No backfill from `learning_log` (design §10).
- **Definitions (design §10):** on day *d*, "đang học" = a row in [*d* − *N* + 1, *d*]; "đã ngừng" = at least one row and none in that window; *N* =
  `learning.active_window_days`. Cohort = registrations of one Monday–Sunday week in `Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh`. D7 / D30 of a cohort = members "đang học" on their own
  registration day + 7 / + 30.
- **Who is counted (E18, B45, B47):** never `admin` / `editor` (role read now, so a later grant removes the past too), never an unverified account, never a deleted or
  deleting one; a member who never learned stays in the cohort's size.
- **Endpoint (design §9 row P6):** `GET /v1/admin/learning/retention?from&to`, `@RequirePermission('ai.view')`, read-only; counts only: no user id, email or
  personal date leaves it (as `dashboard.note`).
- **Migration:** one migration `<timestamp>_nhom_b_m6_learning_activity_days`, timestamp after every migration on `origin/dev` (M1–M3's included).
- **Scale (`ASSUMPTION`, E9):** every read is computed from rows (no aggregate table, no new index beyond the primary key); fine at today's number of learners.
- **Copy:** the design fixes none for M6; the vi copy quoted in M6-T7 is this plan's.
- **PR:** one PR **M6** (T1–T9): the API part is small and the admin card needs it; CI runs once.

**Review focus (M6)** — inputs the design implies but its cases do not name; each has a test in the task shown:
1. A practice answer at 00:01 learner-local after a session opened at 23:58 → the activity row carries the new date, the same as `practice_session_items.local_date`
   (M6-T4).
2. A replayed flashcard review (same `review_id`) or a second write on the same day → still one row and no error; a unique violation would trip the replay `catch` of
   `src/learning/reviews/reviews.service.ts:74-81` (M6-T4, M6-T8).
3. Registered Sunday 23:30 Vietnam time (16:30 UTC, still Sunday) vs Monday 00:00 Vietnam time (17:00 UTC Sunday) → the first in that week's cohort, the second in the
   next (M6-T6, M6-T8).
4. A week nobody registered in → listed with size 0 and "—", never "0%" (M6-T3, M6-T7).
5. The window setting changed to 1 or 60 → the next read applies it to today's counts and to D7 / D30 alike, with nothing stored to recompute (M6-T6, M6-T8).

**Choices this milestone makes where the design leaves room:** activity is written only when the `learning_log` write it marks wrote at least one row (a "Học lại"
with no eligible item, a group delete with no removable item or an add of words already learning writes none); a practice "Bỏ qua" counts (it is a final result,
`writeFinal`), a "try again" does not; deleting a private word neither writes nor erases activity; the registration day is the Vietnam date of `users.created_at`
while activity days are each learner's own local dates (design §10), and "today" is the Vietnam date; a cohort is always a whole week (`from` moves back to its
Monday, the last week is read whole) and every week of the range is listed, oldest first; D7 / D30 are counts (the admin shows the share), `null` until
`week_start + 13` / `week_start + 36` ≤ today and for an empty cohort; today's counts cover every account with a row minus current staff (unverified accounts
cannot sign in, `src/identity/auth/auth.service.ts:150`, so they have no rows); `registrations` leaves out accounts with `deleting_since` set and reads
[`from`, `to`); setting bounds 1–60; missing `from` / `to` → 90 days ending today (the dashboard always sends its own range).

### M6-T1: Table `learning_activity_days`, erased with the account (D39)

### M6-T3: Pure rules — week, window, cohorts, today (design §10, §12)

**Files:** Create `src/learning/retention/retention.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`).

**Interfaces:** Consumes `addDays`, `daysBetween` (`src/common/time/local-date.ts`). Produces (dates are `YYYY-MM-DD`):
- `weekStart(day: string): string` — the Monday of `day`'s week.
- `activeOn(days: ReadonlySet<string>, day: string, n: number): boolean` — some day of [`addDays(day, 1 − n)`, `day`] is in `days`.
- `type Member = { userId: string; regDay: string }`; `type CohortRow = { week_start: string; size: number; d7: number | null; d30: number | null }`;
  `cohortRows(args: { firstWeek: string; lastWeek: string; members: Member[]; activity: Map<string, Set<string>>; today: string; n: number }): CohortRow[]` — one row
  per Monday from `firstWeek` to `lastWeek`; a member belongs to `weekStart(regDay)`; `d7` = members with `activeOn(their days, addDays(regDay, 7), n)`, `null` when
  `addDays(week_start, 13) > today` or `size = 0`; `d30` the same with 30 and 36.
- `todayCounts(args: { inWindow: string[]; ever: string[]; excluded: ReadonlySet<string> }): { active: number; stopped: number }` — active = `inWindow` minus
  `excluded`; stopped = `ever` minus `inWindow` minus `excluded`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing tests** — Lan of design §10 (registers Sunday 01/11, learns 03, 05, 06/11):
  - `weekStart('2026-11-01')` → `'2026-10-26'`; `weekStart('2026-11-02')` → `'2026-11-02'`; `weekStart('2027-01-01')` → `'2026-12-28'`.
  - `activeOn(LAN, '2026-11-08', 7)` → `true` (her D7); `'2026-11-12'` → `true`; `'2026-11-13'` → `false` ("đã ngừng" from 13/11); `'2026-12-01'` → `false` (her
    D30). Edges (design §12): `activeOn({'2026-11-02'}, '2026-11-08', 7)` → `true` (*d* − 6); `activeOn({'2026-11-01'}, '2026-11-08', 7)` → `false` (*d* − 7);
    `n = 1`: only `'2026-11-08'` itself counts.
  - `cohortRows` with week `'2026-10-26'`, members Lan (`'2026-11-01'`) and Minh (`'2026-10-26'`, no row, B45): today `'2026-12-15'` → `[{ week_start: '2026-10-26',
    size: 2, d7: 1, d30: 0 }]`; today `'2026-11-07'` → `d7: null, d30: null` (B46); `'2026-11-08'` → `d7: 1, d30: null`; `'2026-12-01'` → `d30: 0`. Weeks
    `'2026-10-26'`…`'2026-11-02'` with nobody in the second → its row `{ week_start: '2026-11-02', size: 0, d7: null, d30: null }` (Review focus 4).
  - `todayCounts({ inWindow: ['a', 'b', 'e'], ever: ['a', 'b', 'c', 'e', 'f'], excluded: new Set(['e', 'f']) })` → `{ active: 2, stopped: 1 }`; all empty →
    `{ active: 0, stopped: 0 }`.
- [ ] **Step 2: Run** `pnpm --filter api test src/learning/retention/retention.spec.ts` → FAIL (module missing). **Step 3: Implement.** **Step 4: Run** → PASS.

### M6-T6: Retention report and `GET /v1/admin/learning/retention` (design §9 row P6, §10, E9, E18)

**Files:** Create `src/learning/retention/retention.service.ts` (+ spec), `src/learning/retention/admin-retention.controller.ts` (+ spec); modify
`src/learning/learning.module.ts`, `src/learning/learning.constants.ts` (`RETENTION_DAY_ZONE = 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh'`, `RETENTION_DEFAULT_DAYS = 90`).

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `dayRange` (`src/common/time/day-range.ts:11`), `localDate`, `zonedMidnight`, `addDays`, `dateValue`, `dateString` (`src/common/time/local-date.ts`),
  M6-T3's rules, `IdentityService.registrations` (M6-T5), `IdentityService.staffUserIds` (M4-T3), `SettingsService.get().learning.activeWindowDays` (M6-T2).
- Produces:
  - `RetentionView = { from: string; to: string; window_days: number; today: { date: string; active: number; stopped: number }; cohorts: CohortRow[] }`.
  - `RetentionService.report(query: { from?: string; to?: string }, now: Date): Promise<RetentionView>` — `range = dayRange(query, RETENTION_DAY_ZONE, now,
    RETENTION_DEFAULT_DAYS)` (400 `validation-failed` for a reversed or > 366-day range, as today); `firstWeek = weekStart(range.from)`, `lastWeek =
    weekStart(range.to)`; members = `registrations(zonedMidnight(zone, firstWeek), zonedMidnight(zone, addDays(lastWeek, 7)))` kept when `emailVerified && !staff`,
    `regDay = localDate(zone, createdAt)`; one read of the members' rows with `local_date` in [`addDays(firstWeek, 8 − N)`, `addDays(lastWeek, 36)`] (none when there
    is no member); `today = localDate(zone, now)`; one read of distinct `user_id` with a row in [`addDays(today, 1 − N)`, `today`], one of every distinct `user_id`;
    `excluded = new Set(await staffUserIds())`; the view's `from` = `firstWeek`, `to` = `range.to`.
  - `AdminRetentionController`: `@Controller('admin/learning/retention')`, `@RequirePermission('ai.view')`, `@Get()` with `rangeQuery = z.strictObject({ from:
    z.iso.date().optional(), to: z.iso.date().optional() })` (as `src/practice/reports/admin-ai-quality.controller.ts:7`) → `report(query, clock.now())`.

### M6-T8: `pnpm smoke:nhom-b`, part M6 (design §13 "đọc dashboard và D7/D30")

**Files:** Modify `apps/api/scripts/smoke-nhom-b.mjs` (add `async function partM6()` after M5's part and call it; its header names the psql seeding below).

**Interfaces:** Consumes the script's helpers (`step`, `ok`, `check`, `want`, `wantProblem`, `psql`), the editor E of `partM4()`, the catalog verbs W1–W3 of
`setup()`, the practice helpers M4's part uses (as `scripts/smoke-buoc-7.mjs:304-320`); acts as a new admin (Global Constraints). The API stamps `users.created_at` and
every activity date with the clock, so past registrations and past learning days are **inserted with psql**: users as `scripts/smoke-buoc-8.mjs:127-137` (`sqlUsers`)
does, with a given `created_at`, `timezone 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh'`, no password (they never sign in), emails `smokenb-m6-<label>-<run>@example.com`; days with `INSERT INTO
learning_activity_days`. Every other account of the smoke DB was made today, so the two seeded weeks hold only the seeded accounts. T = today in Vietnam, M0 its
Monday, A = M0 − 49 days, B = A + 7; `vn(day, 'hh:mm')` = that Vietnam wall time.

Steps of `partM6()`:
1. Setting and access: `GET /v1/admin/settings` → `learning.active_window_days` 7, bound `{ min: 1, max: 60 }`; E (`ai.view`) `GET …/retention?from=T&to=T` → 200,
   keys exactly `from, to, window_days, today, cohorts`; `from` = T + 1, `to` = T → 400 `validation-failed`.
2. B51 (D39) through the real write paths: learner L6 made with `POST /v1/admin/users` (`staff_role: null`, `Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh`) and signed in; psql: no row for L6.
   L6's `GET …/retention` → 403 `forbidden`. L6 makes a group, adds W1–W3 (`POST /v1/groups/:id/items`), answers one flashcard (`GET /v1/groups/:id/queue`,
   `POST /v1/reviews`), then a non-AI practice session on the group with three final answers → psql: L6 has exactly **one** row, `local_date` T, while
   `learning_log` holds 4 rows of L6 (3 `item_added`, 1 `review`: the writes happened). psql deletes that row; one more final practice answer → the row is back
   (a practice answer alone writes it). L6 quick-adds a private word (`POST /v1/quick-add`) and deletes it (`DELETE /v1/my-words/:entry_id`) → still one row, and
   psql finds no `learning_log` row of that sense (the delete ran).
3. Seed (psql): week A — `a1` `vn(A, '10:00')` with days A+3, A+5, A+6; `a2` `vn(A+2, '10:00')` with A+9, A+32; `a3` `vn(A+4, '10:00')`, no day (B45); `a4`
   `vn(A+6, '23:30')` with A+7; `a5` `vn(A+1, '10:00')` with A+1; `e1` `vn(A+1, '10:00')`, `staff_role 'editor'`, with A+8; `u1` `vn(A+1, '10:00')`,
   `email_verified_at NULL`. Week B — `b1` `vn(B, '00:00')` with B+7.
4. Cohorts: `GET …/retention?from=A+2&to=B+6` → `from` A, `to` B+6, `cohorts` deep-equal `[{ week_start: A, size: 5, d7: 3, d30: 1 }, { week_start: B, size: 1, d7: 1,
   d30: 0 }]` (a1, a2, a4 at +7 — a2 on the day itself, a4 on *d* − 6 —, not a5 on *d* − 7; a2 at +30; a4 at Sunday 23:30 in A, b1 at Monday 00:00 in B; e1, u1 out).
5. B46: `?from=M0&to=T` → one cohort, `week_start` M0, `d7: null`, `d30: null`, `size` = psql count of users with `created_at ≥ vn(M0, '00:00')`, `staff_role IS NULL`,
   `email_verified_at IS NOT NULL`, `deleting_since IS NULL`.
6. B47: `PUT /v1/admin/users/<a1>/staff-role { staff_role: 'editor' }` → step 4's read gives week A `{ size: 4, d7: 2, d30: 1 }`; back to `null` → `{ size: 5, d7: 3,
   d30: 1 }`.
7. Today and *N* (Review focus 5): `today.date` = T; `today.active` / `today.stopped` = psql counts of distinct `user_id` whose user has `staff_role IS NULL`, with a row in
   [T − 6, T] / with rows but none there. `PUT /v1/admin/settings { version, values: { learning: { active_window_days: 60 } } }` → `window_days` 60, `today.active` = its value before the change + 5
   and `today.stopped` − 5 (a1, a2, a4, a5, b1 fall inside [T − 59, T]), week A `{ size: 5, d7: 4, d30: 4 }`; back to 7.
8. Deletion: `POST /v1/admin/users/<b1>/delete { confirm_email }` → 204; psql: no `learning_activity_days` row of b1; step 4's read gives week B `{ week_start: B, size: 0,
   d7: null, d30: null }`.

- [ ] **Step 1:** Write `partM6()`. **Step 2:** Run it as the smoke run (Global Constraints) — every step `ok`. A step failing on a code defect: red unit test in the
  owning task's spec, then the fix (two commits), then the smoke again.
- [ ] **Step 3: Commit** `test(api): add the d7 d30 part of the nhom b smoke`.

- [ ] **Step 2:** The smoke run (Global Constraints) — passes; the old smokes and `pnpm learner:setup` pass. Because M6 adds a write to every learner write path and a

## Closing — after M6 (design §13 "Xong khi")

Branch `docs/nhom-b-as-built` from `origin/dev` after M6 merged; C-T1 and C-T2 go in one PR into `dev`. C-T3 is the owner's step.

### C-T1: Final smoke and old smokes on fresh databases

- [ ] **Step 1:** The smoke run (Global Constraints) with every part, `partM1()` … `partM6()` — passes. Replace `docs/deliveries/nhom-b-smoke-output.txt` with the output of
  the second run under the heading "Nhóm B — final run <date>" (contract §7).
- [ ] **Step 2:** Old smokes (design §13): `pnpm smoke:buoc-6`, `pnpm smoke:buoc-7`, each on a fresh database with its documented env, and `pnpm learner:setup -- --admin
  <email> --learner <email> --plan pro` on a fresh DB, then again on the same DB — all exit 0.
- [ ] **Step 3: Commit** `docs(nhom-b): record the final smoke output`.

### C-T2: As-built record

- [ ] **Step 1:** Append "# Nhóm B — as-built (<date>)" to `docs/deliveries/nhom-b.md`: per milestone the PRs, the test counts (api and admin unit, mocked and real-stack
  e2e), the decisions taken while building and where they are written, what differs from this plan, and what is still open (C-T3; the note under "Open owner
  questions" on hiding a model by hand, unless the owner ruled on it). In `CLAUDE.md` add one pointer line "Nhóm B — plan & as-built" to that file (no content copied).
- [ ] **Step 2: Commit** `docs(nhom-b): add the as-built record`; push; open the PR into `dev`; merge when CI is green.

### C-T3: The owner's real run (owner step; design §13, D30)

Claude does not run this: it uses the owner's own keys, with a budget of **5 USD**.
- [ ] **Step 1 (owner):** start the API and worker with the owner's `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` (and the key of the provider that writes the questions), `AI_FAKE_ENABLED=false`, and
  `ai.admin_daily_usd_cap` set so the run stays under 5 USD.
- [ ] **Step 2 (owner):** for `generate_cloze`: freeze a version that has held-out examples; eval the live config on it; give "Chỉ một đáp án" one Anthropic model,
  "Nhân bản judge" and give the clone a second Anthropic model (D3); judge the run with both; label 30 outputs on "Gắn nhãn" (keys 1 / 2 / 3); read "Độ khớp" of each judge.
- [ ] **Step 3:** Claude adds the numbers the owner reports (cost, agreement of each judge, calibrated or not) to the as-built in `docs/deliveries/nhom-b.md`; commit
  `docs(nhom-b): record the owner's real run` on a `docs/` branch, PR into `dev`.
